# A2 Vocabulary — bản xem trước

Tổng: **2000 từ/cụm từ** trong **25 chủ đề** (100 bài học).

## Đời sống hằng ngày

`doi-song-hang-ngay` · Từ vựng về thói quen buổi sáng, việc nhà, lịch trình và cách thư giãn vào cuối tuần.

### Bài 1. Buổi sáng & thói quen cá nhân

_20 từ/cụm từ về các bước chuẩn bị buổi sáng, đồ dùng cá nhân và cách nói về đúng giờ hay trễ giờ._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **alarm** | /əˈlɑːm/ | n | đồng hồ báo thức | a clock that wakes you at a set time | I set my alarm for six o'clock. |
| 2 | **get dressed** | /ˌɡet ˈdrest/ | phrase | mặc quần áo | to put on your clothes | It takes me ten minutes to get dressed. |
| 3 | **shower** | /ˈʃaʊə(r)/ | n | vòi sen; việc tắm | a wash under falling water | I take a quick shower before breakfast. |
| 4 | **shave** | /ʃeɪv/ | v | cạo râu | to cut hair off your face | He shaves every morning before work. |
| 5 | **comb** | /kəʊm/ | n/v | lược; chải tóc | a tool for arranging your hair; to arrange your hair | She combed her hair in front of the mirror. |
| 6 | **mirror** | /ˈmɪrə(r)/ | n | gương | glass that reflects an image | The bathroom mirror was covered in steam. |
| 7 | **toothpaste** | /ˈtuːθpeɪst/ | n | kem đánh răng | a paste used for cleaning teeth | We are running out of toothpaste. |
| 8 | **toothbrush** | /ˈtuːθbrʌʃ/ | n | bàn chải đánh răng | a small brush for cleaning teeth | Replace your toothbrush every three months. |
| 9 | **towel** | /ˈtaʊəl/ | n | khăn tắm | a cloth used for drying your body | Grab a clean towel from the cupboard. |
| 10 | **soap** | /səʊp/ | n | xà phòng | a substance used for washing | Wash your hands with soap and water. |
| 11 | **shampoo** | /ʃæmˈpuː/ | n | dầu gội | a liquid used for washing hair | This shampoo is gentle on dry hair. |
| 12 | **routine** | /ruːˈtiːn/ | n | thói quen, trình tự hằng ngày | the usual order in which you do things | Exercise is part of my morning routine. |
| 13 | **habit** | /ˈhæbɪt/ | n | thói quen | something you do regularly | Reading before bed is a good habit. |
| 14 | **early** | /ˈɜːli/ | adj/adv | sớm | before the usual or expected time | I usually get up early on weekdays. |
| 15 | **late** | /leɪt/ | adj/adv | muộn, trễ | after the expected time | Sorry, I'm late — the bus didn't come. |
| 16 | **on time** | /ɒn ˈtaɪm/ | phrase | đúng giờ | at the planned time | The train arrived exactly on time. |
| 17 | **oversleep** | /ˌəʊvəˈsliːp/ | v | ngủ quên | to sleep longer than you intended | I overslept and missed breakfast. |
| 18 | **punctual** | /ˈpʌŋktʃuəl/ | adj | đúng giờ, chuyên cần | always arriving at the right time | She is always punctual for meetings. |
| 19 | **wash** | /wɒʃ/ | v | rửa, giặt | to clean something with water | Wash your face with warm water. |
| 20 | **dry** | /draɪ/ | v/adj | lau khô; khô | to remove water from something; not wet | Dry your hands on the towel. |

### Bài 2. Việc nhà & sinh hoạt

_20 từ/cụm từ về công việc nhà, dọn dẹp, giặt giũ, sửa chữa và chăm sóc đồ đạc trong nhà._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **chore** | /tʃɔː(r)/ | n | việc vặt trong nhà | a small regular job around the house | The children have a list of chores. |
| 2 | **housework** | /ˈhaʊswɜːk/ | n | công việc nhà | the work of cleaning and tidying a house | We share the housework equally. |
| 3 | **tidy** | /ˈtaɪdi/ | v/adj | dọn dẹp; gọn gàng | to make a place neat; neat | Please tidy your room before dinner. |
| 4 | **clean** | /kliːn/ | v/adj | lau chùi; sạch | to remove dirt; free from dirt | I clean the kitchen every evening. |
| 5 | **sweep** | /swiːp/ | v | quét | to clean the floor with a brush | Sweep the floor after cooking. |
| 6 | **mop** | /mɒp/ | v/n | lau nhà; cây lau nhà | to wash a floor with water; a tool for this | Mop the bathroom floor once a week. |
| 7 | **vacuum** | /ˈvækjuːm/ | v/n | hút bụi; máy hút bụi | to clean with a vacuum cleaner; the machine | I vacuum the living room on Saturdays. |
| 8 | **dust** | /dʌst/ | n/v | bụi; phủi bụi | fine dirt; to remove dust from something | There is dust on the shelves. |
| 9 | **laundry** | /ˈlɔːndri/ | n | quần áo cần giặt; việc giặt giũ | clothes to be washed; the act of washing them | I do the laundry on Sunday morning. |
| 10 | **iron** | /ˈaɪən/ | v/n | là (ủi); bàn là | to press clothes smooth; the tool for this | Can you iron my shirt for the interview? |
| 11 | **fold** | /fəʊld/ | v | gấp (quần áo) | to bend clothes so they are flat | Fold the towels and put them away. |
| 12 | **dishwasher** | /ˈdɪʃwɒʃə(r)/ | n | máy rửa bát | a machine that washes dishes | Load the dishwasher after dinner. |
| 13 | **washing machine** | /ˈwɒʃɪŋ məʃiːn/ | n | máy giặt | a machine that washes clothes | The washing machine is broken again. |
| 14 | **rubbish** | /ˈrʌbɪʃ/ | n | rác | things you throw away | Put the rubbish in the bin outside. |
| 15 | **recycle** | /ˌriːˈsaɪkl/ | v | tái chế | to process waste so it can be used again | We recycle glass, paper and plastic. |
| 16 | **repair** | /rɪˈpeə(r)/ | v/n | sửa chữa | to fix something that is damaged | It costs a lot to repair the old fridge. |
| 17 | **broken** | /ˈbrəʊkən/ | adj | bị hỏng | damaged and not working | The vacuum cleaner is broken. |
| 18 | **clear up** | /ˌklɪər ˈʌp/ | phr v | dọn dẹp | to tidy a place after use | Let's clear up the kitchen together. |
| 19 | **take out** | /ˌteɪk ˈaʊt/ | phr v | mang ra ngoài (đổ rác) | to remove something and put it outside | Don't forget to take out the rubbish. |
| 20 | **look after** | /ˌlʊk ˈɑːftə(r)/ | phr v | chăm sóc, trông nom | to take care of someone or something | She looks after her younger brother. |

### Bài 3. Lịch trình & thời gian

_20 từ/cụm từ về lịch làm việc, cuộc hẹn, hạn chót và cách nói về việc thay đổi kế hoạch._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **schedule** | /ˈʃedjuːl/ | n/v | lịch trình; lên lịch | a plan of times and activities; to plan something | My schedule is full this week. |
| 2 | **timetable** | /ˈtaɪmteɪbl/ | n | thời khóa biểu, lịch trình | a list of times when things happen | Check the bus timetable before you leave. |
| 3 | **appointment** | /əˈpɔɪntmənt/ | n | cuộc hẹn | an arranged meeting at a fixed time | I have a dentist appointment at three. |
| 4 | **calendar** | /ˈkælɪndə(r)/ | n | lịch | a chart showing days, weeks and months | Mark the deadline on your calendar. |
| 5 | **deadline** | /ˈdedlaɪn/ | n | hạn chót | the latest time to finish something | The deadline for the report is Friday. |
| 6 | **busy** | /ˈbɪzi/ | adj | bận rộn | having a lot to do | She is busy preparing for the exam. |
| 7 | **free** | /friː/ | adj | rảnh; miễn phí | not busy; costing nothing | Are you free on Saturday afternoon? |
| 8 | **available** | /əˈveɪləbl/ | adj | có sẵn, rảnh | able to be used or met | The doctor is available after lunch. |
| 9 | **delay** | /dɪˈleɪ/ | n/v | sự trì hoãn; hoãn, làm trễ | a period of waiting; to make something late | The flight has a two-hour delay. |
| 10 | **cancel** | /ˈkænsl/ | v | huỷ | to decide that something will not happen | They had to cancel the meeting. |
| 11 | **postpone** | /pəˈspəʊn/ | v | hoãn lại | to move something to a later time | We decided to postpone the trip until May. |
| 12 | **arrange** | /əˈreɪndʒ/ | v | sắp xếp | to plan or organize something | Can you arrange a meeting for Monday? |
| 13 | **remind** | /rɪˈmaɪnd/ | v | nhắc nhở | to make someone remember something | Please remind me to call my parents. |
| 14 | **noon** | /nuːn/ | n | buổi trưa (12 giờ) | twelve o'clock in the middle of the day | The shop opens at noon. |
| 15 | **midnight** | /ˈmɪdnaɪt/ | n | nửa đêm (12 giờ đêm) | twelve o'clock at night | He finished the essay at midnight. |
| 16 | **weekday** | /ˈwiːkdeɪ/ | n | ngày trong tuần | any day from Monday to Friday | I work out on weekdays. |
| 17 | **weekend** | /ˌwiːkˈend/ | n | cuối tuần | Saturday and Sunday | We usually visit our grandparents at the weekend. |
| 18 | **monthly** | /ˈmʌnθli/ | adj/adv | hằng tháng | happening once a month | The team has a monthly meeting. |
| 19 | **daily** | /ˈdeɪli/ | adj/adv | hằng ngày | happening every day | The app tracks my daily progress. |
| 20 | **flexible** | /ˈfleksəbl/ | adj | linh hoạt | able to change easily to suit the situation | My working hours are quite flexible. |

### Bài 4. Cuối tuần & thư giãn

_20 từ/cụm từ về hoạt động thư giãn, sở thích, sự kiện văn hoá và cách hẹn gặp bạn bè._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **relax** | /rɪˈlæks/ | v | thư giãn | to rest and become less tense | I relax by listening to music. |
| 2 | **unwind** | /ˌʌnˈwaɪnd/ | v | thư giãn, xả hơi | to relax after work or stress | A hot bath helps me unwind after work. |
| 3 | **leisure** | /ˈleʒə(r)/ | n | thời gian rảnh | time when you are not working | She enjoys reading in her leisure time. |
| 4 | **picnic** | /ˈpɪknɪk/ | n | chuyến dã ngoại | a meal eaten outdoors | We had a picnic in the park. |
| 5 | **barbecue** | /ˈbɑːbɪkjuː/ | n | tiệc nướng | an outdoor meal with grilled food | They invited us to a barbecue on Sunday. |
| 6 | **camping** | /ˈkæmpɪŋ/ | n | việc cắm trại | staying outdoors in a tent | We go camping in the mountains every summer. |
| 7 | **hike** | /haɪk/ | n/v | chuyến đi bộ đường dài; đi bộ đường dài | a long walk in the countryside; to take one | The hike took us four hours. |
| 8 | **jog** | /dʒɒɡ/ | v/n | chạy bộ nhẹ; buổi chạy bộ | to run slowly for exercise; a slow run | I jog around the lake before breakfast. |
| 9 | **gym** | /dʒɪm/ | n | phòng tập thể hình | a place with equipment for exercise | He goes to the gym three times a week. |
| 10 | **yoga** | /ˈjəʊɡə/ | n | yoga | a system of exercises for body and mind | Yoga helps me reduce stress. |
| 11 | **collect** | /kəˈlekt/ | v | sưu tầm, thu gom | to gather things as a hobby | My brother collects old coins. |
| 12 | **collection** | /kəˈlekʃn/ | n | bộ sưu tập | a group of similar things kept together | She showed me her stamp collection. |
| 13 | **photography** | /fəˈtɒɡrəfi/ | n | nhiếp ảnh | the art of taking photographs | Photography is his favourite hobby. |
| 14 | **board game** | /ˈbɔːd ɡeɪm/ | n | trò chơi trên bàn | a game played on a board | We played a board game after dinner. |
| 15 | **puzzle** | /ˈpʌzl/ | n | trò xếp hình; câu đố | a game or question that tests thinking | The children finished the puzzle together. |
| 16 | **concert** | /ˈkɒnsət/ | n | buổi hòa nhạc | a public performance of music | We bought tickets for a rock concert. |
| 17 | **exhibition** | /ˌeksɪˈbɪʃn/ | n | triển lãm | a public show of art or objects | There is an art exhibition at the museum. |
| 18 | **festival** | /ˈfestɪvl/ | n | lễ hội | a public event with music, food or culture | The street festival lasts for three days. |
| 19 | **neighbourhood** | /ˈneɪbəhʊd/ | n | khu phố | the area around where you live | Our neighbourhood is quiet and friendly. |
| 20 | **catch up** | /ˌkætʃ ˈʌp/ | phr v | gặp gỡ trò chuyện; cập nhật | to meet someone and share recent news | Let's catch up over coffee next week. |

## Gia đình & các mối quan hệ

`gia-dinh-moi-quan-he` · Từ vựng về thành viên gia đình, các giai đoạn cuộc đời, tính cách và những dịp sum họp.

### Bài 1. Thành viên trong gia đình

_20 từ/cụm từ chỉ người thân, quan hệ hôn nhân, anh chị em ruột thịt và họ hàng hai bên._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **relative** | /ˈrelətɪv/ | n | người thân, họ hàng | a member of your family | Many relatives came to the wedding. |
| 2 | **relationship** | /rɪˈleɪʃnʃɪp/ | n | mối quan hệ | the way people are connected | She has a close relationship with her sister. |
| 3 | **parent** | /ˈpeərənt/ | n | bố hoặc mẹ | a mother or father | Every parent wants their child to be happy. |
| 4 | **partner** | /ˈpɑːtnə(r)/ | n | bạn đời, người yêu | the person you live with or love | He came to the party with his partner. |
| 5 | **spouse** | /spaʊs/ | n | vợ hoặc chồng | a husband or wife | You can bring your spouse to the dinner. |
| 6 | **sibling** | /ˈsɪblɪŋ/ | n | anh/chị/em ruột | a brother or sister | I have two siblings, both older than me. |
| 7 | **stepmother** | /ˈstepmʌðə(r)/ | n | mẹ kế | your father's new wife | His stepmother treats him kindly. |
| 8 | **stepfather** | /ˈstepfɑːðə(r)/ | n | bố dượng | your mother's new husband | My stepfather taught me to ride a bike. |
| 9 | **stepbrother** | /ˈstepbrʌðə(r)/ | n | anh/em trai kế | the son of your step-parent | My stepbrother is the same age as me. |
| 10 | **stepsister** | /ˈstepsɪstə(r)/ | n | chị/em gái kế | the daughter of your step-parent | Her stepsister lives in Canada. |
| 11 | **half-brother** | /ˈhɑːf brʌðə(r)/ | n | anh/em trai cùng cha khác mẹ | a brother who shares one parent with you | I have a half-brother from my father's first marriage. |
| 12 | **half-sister** | /ˈhɑːf sɪstə(r)/ | n | chị/em gái cùng cha khác mẹ | a sister who shares one parent with you | My half-sister visits us every summer. |
| 13 | **mother-in-law** | /ˈmʌðər ɪn lɔː/ | n | mẹ chồng, mẹ vợ | the mother of your husband or wife | My mother-in-law cooks very well. |
| 14 | **father-in-law** | /ˈfɑːðər ɪn lɔː/ | n | bố chồng, bố vợ | the father of your husband or wife | Her father-in-law is a retired teacher. |
| 15 | **brother-in-law** | /ˈbrʌðər ɪn lɔː/ | n | anh/em rể, anh/em vợ/chồng | the brother of your spouse, or your sister's husband | My brother-in-law works at a bank. |
| 16 | **sister-in-law** | /ˈsɪstər ɪn lɔː/ | n | chị/em dâu, chị/em vợ/chồng | the sister of your spouse, or your brother's wife | My sister-in-law is coming for dinner. |
| 17 | **nephew** | /ˈnefjuː/ | n | cháu trai (con anh/chị/em) | the son of your brother or sister | My nephew starts school this year. |
| 18 | **niece** | /niːs/ | n | cháu gái (con anh/chị/em) | the daughter of your brother or sister | I bought a gift for my niece. |
| 19 | **only child** | /ˌəʊnli ˈtʃaɪld/ | n | con một | a child with no brothers or sisters | As an only child, she spent a lot of time alone. |
| 20 | **generation** | /ˌdʒenəˈreɪʃn/ | n | thế hệ | all the people born around the same time | Three generations live in that house. |

### Bài 2. Các giai đoạn của cuộc đời

_20 từ/cụm từ về sinh ra, tuổi thơ, tuổi trưởng thành, nuôi dạy con và tuổi già._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **birth** | /bɜːθ/ | n | sự sinh ra | the time when a baby is born | The birth of their daughter changed their lives. |
| 2 | **newborn** | /ˈnjuːbɔːn/ | adj/n | sơ sinh; trẻ sơ sinh | a baby that has just been born | The newborn baby slept peacefully. |
| 3 | **childhood** | /ˈtʃaɪldhʊd/ | n | tuổi thơ | the time when you are a child | She had a happy childhood in the countryside. |
| 4 | **upbringing** | /ˈʌpbrɪŋɪŋ/ | n | cách nuôi dạy | the way your parents raise you | His strict upbringing taught him discipline. |
| 5 | **teenager** | /ˈtiːneɪdʒə(r)/ | n | thiếu niên (13–19 tuổi) | a person between 13 and 19 years old | As a teenager, he loved playing football. |
| 6 | **adolescence** | /ˌædəˈlesns/ | n | tuổi vị thành niên | the period between childhood and adulthood | Adolescence can be a difficult time for many young people. |
| 7 | **adult** | /ˈædʌlt/ | n/adj | người trưởng thành | a fully grown person | Adults pay a higher ticket price. |
| 8 | **adulthood** | /ˈædʌlthʊd/ | n | tuổi trưởng thành | the period when you are an adult | She moved abroad at the start of adulthood. |
| 9 | **middle-aged** | /ˌmɪdl ˈeɪdʒd/ | adj | trung niên | between about 45 and 65 years old | My middle-aged uncle has started running marathons. |
| 10 | **elderly** | /ˈeldəli/ | adj | cao tuổi | old, used politely | An elderly couple lives next door. |
| 11 | **retirement** | /rɪˈtaɪəmənt/ | n | sự nghỉ hưu | the time after you stop working | He is enjoying his retirement. |
| 12 | **retire** | /rɪˈtaɪə(r)/ | v | nghỉ hưu | to stop working because of age | My mother retired last year. |
| 13 | **grow up** | /ˌɡrəʊ ˈʌp/ | phr v | lớn lên | to become an adult | I grew up in a quiet neighbourhood. |
| 14 | **bring up** | /ˌbrɪŋ ˈʌp/ | phr v | nuôi dạy | to care for and educate a child | They brought up three children. |
| 15 | **raise** | /reɪz/ | v | nuôi dạy; nâng lên | to care for a child until adulthood | It is not easy to raise a family. |
| 16 | **twin** | /twɪn/ | n/adj | cặp sinh đôi; sinh đôi | one of two children born at the same time | The twins look exactly the same. |
| 17 | **adoption** | /əˈdɒpʃn/ | n | sự nhận nuôi | the legal act of taking another person's child as your own | They are going through the adoption process. |
| 18 | **pregnant** | /ˈpreɡnənt/ | adj | mang thai | expecting a baby | My sister is six months pregnant. |
| 19 | **mature** | /məˈtʃʊə(r)/ | adj | trưởng thành, chín chắn | behaving in a sensible adult way | He is very mature for his age. |
| 20 | **generation gap** | /ˌdʒenəˈreɪʃn ɡæp/ | n | khoảng cách thế hệ | differences in attitudes between old and young people | The family often talks about the generation gap. |

### Bài 3. Tính cách trong gia đình

_20 tính từ và cụm từ mô tả cách các thành viên đối xử, quan tâm và gắn bó với nhau._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **caring** | /ˈkeərɪŋ/ | adj | hay quan tâm | kind and looking after other people | She is a caring mother and friend. |
| 2 | **supportive** | /səˈpɔːtɪv/ | adj | luôn ủng hộ | giving help and encouragement | My family has always been supportive of my studies. |
| 3 | **strict** | /strɪkt/ | adj | nghiêm khắc | expecting rules to be followed | My father was strict about homework. |
| 4 | **lenient** | /ˈliːniənt/ | adj | dễ dãi, khoan dung | not strict in punishment | The children prefer their lenient uncle. |
| 5 | **tolerant** | /ˈtɒlərənt/ | adj | bao dung, kiên nhẫn | willing to accept differences and mistakes | She is very tolerant of other people's mistakes. |
| 6 | **easy-going** | /ˌiːzi ˈɡəʊɪŋ/ | adj | dễ tính | relaxed and not easily worried | My easy-going brother never argues. |
| 7 | **responsible** | /rɪˈspɒnsəbl/ | adj | có trách nhiệm | reliable and sensible | She is the most responsible person in the family. |
| 8 | **reliable** | /rɪˈlaɪəbl/ | adj | đáng tin cậy | able to be trusted | You can always count on my reliable cousin. |
| 9 | **protective** | /prəˈtektɪv/ | adj | hay che chở | wanting to keep someone safe | Older brothers can be very protective. |
| 10 | **affectionate** | /əˈfekʃənət/ | adj | trìu mến | showing love in a physical or kind way | My grandparents are very affectionate with each other. |
| 11 | **loving** | /ˈlʌvɪŋ/ | adj | yêu thương | showing love | She grew up in a loving home. |
| 12 | **devoted** | /dɪˈvəʊtɪd/ | adj | hết lòng, tận tụy | giving a lot of love and time to someone | He is a devoted father. |
| 13 | **loyal** | /ˈlɔɪəl/ | adj | trung thành | always supporting your family or friends | She is loyal to her friends. |
| 14 | **selfish** | /ˈselfɪʃ/ | adj | ích kỷ | thinking only about yourself | It was selfish of him not to help. |
| 15 | **jealous** | /ˈdʒeləs/ | adj | ghen tị | unhappy because someone has something you want | He gets jealous when his sister gets attention. |
| 16 | **understanding** | /ˌʌndəˈstændɪŋ/ | adj | thấu hiểu | kind and able to forgive | My mother is very understanding. |
| 17 | **demanding** | /dɪˈmɑːndɪŋ/ | adj | hay đòi hỏi | expecting a lot of effort or attention | Her demanding job leaves little family time. |
| 18 | **traditional** | /trəˈdɪʃənl/ | adj | theo truyền thống | following old customs | They have a traditional family dinner on Sundays. |
| 19 | **close-knit** | /ˌkləʊs ˈnɪt/ | adj | gắn bó chặt chẽ | having strong relationships with each other | It is a close-knit community. |
| 20 | **role model** | /ˈrəʊl mɒdl/ | n | tấm gương, hình mẫu | a person you admire and want to be like | My grandfather is my role model. |

_20 từ/cụm từ về đám cưới, giỗ chạp, sum họp, phong tục và cách các thế hệ giúp đỡ nhau._

| 11 | **tradition** | /trəˈdɪʃn/ | n | truyền thống | a custom passed down over time | It is a family tradition to cook together. |

| 18 | **make up** | /ˌmeɪk ˈʌp/ | phr v | làm hoà | to become friends again after an argument | They argued but made up the next day. |
| 19 | **share** | /ʃeə(r)/ | v | chia sẻ | to give part of something to others | The sisters share a bedroom. |
| 20 | **babysit** | /ˈbeɪbisɪt/ | v | trông trẻ | to look after a child while the parents are out | My aunt babysits us on Friday evenings. |

## Bạn bè & giao tiếp xã hội

`ban-be-giao-tiep` · Từ vựng để làm quen, rủ bạn đi chơi, giữ liên lạc và xử lý những bất đồng trong quan hệ.

### Bài 1. Làm quen & gặp gỡ

_20 từ/cụm từ về người quen, đồng nghiệp, cách chào hỏi và tạo ấn tượng ban đầu._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **acquaintance** | /əˈkweɪntəns/ | n | người quen | someone you know but not well | She is an acquaintance from my old job. |
| 2 | **stranger** | /ˈstreɪndʒə(r)/ | n | người lạ | a person you do not know | Do not share personal details with strangers. |
| 3 | **classmate** | /ˈklɑːsmeɪt/ | n | bạn cùng lớp | someone in the same class as you | I met my best friend when we were classmates. |
| 4 | **colleague** | /ˈkɒliːɡ/ | n | đồng nghiệp | a person you work with | My colleagues organised a leaving party. |
| 5 | **neighbour** | /ˈneɪbə(r)/ | n | hàng xóm | a person who lives near you | Our neighbours invited us for tea. |
| 6 | **mate** | /meɪt/ | n | bạn thân (thân mật) | a friend, especially in informal British English | He is an old mate from university. |
| 7 | **buddy** | /ˈbʌdi/ | n | bạn thân (Mỹ) | a close friend, informal | My gym buddy keeps me motivated. |
| 8 | **companion** | /kəmˈpæniən/ | n | bạn đồng hành | a person who spends time with you | A dog can be a loyal companion. |
| 9 | **get on well** | /ˌɡet ɒn ˈwel/ | phrase | hợp nhau, có quan hệ tốt | to have a good relationship | I get on well with my flatmate. |
| 10 | **hit it off** | /ˌhɪt ɪt ˈɒf/ | idiom | hợp nhau ngay từ đầu | to like each other immediately | They hit it off at the party. |
| 11 | **approach** | /əˈprəʊtʃ/ | v | tiếp cận, đến gần | to come near or speak to someone | He approached me and said hello. |
| 12 | **greet** | /ɡriːt/ | v | chào hỏi | to say hello to someone | She greeted every guest at the door. |
| 13 | **handshake** | /ˈhændʃeɪk/ | n | cái bắt tay | the act of shaking hands | A firm handshake makes a good impression. |
| 14 | **eye contact** | /ˈaɪ kɒntækt/ | n | giao tiếp bằng mắt | looking directly into someone's eyes | Good eye contact shows confidence. |
| 15 | **small talk** | /ˈsmɔːl tɔːk/ | n | chuyện xã giao | polite conversation about unimportant things | We made small talk while waiting for the bus. |
| 16 | **compliment** | /ˈkɒmplɪmənt/ | n/v | lời khen; khen | a nice remark about someone | She paid me a compliment on my cooking. |
| 17 | **invite** | /ɪnˈvaɪt/ | v | mời | to ask someone to come to an event | They invited us to their new flat. |
| 18 | **invitation** | /ˌɪnvɪˈteɪʃn/ | n | lời mời | a written or spoken request to attend | I received an invitation to the wedding. |
| 19 | **host** | /həʊst/ | n/v | chủ nhà; đăng cai tổ chức | a person who invites guests | Our host made everyone feel welcome. |
| 20 | **hostess** | /ˈhəʊstəs/ | n | nữ chủ nhà | a woman who invites and looks after guests | The hostess introduced the guests to each other. |

### Bài 2. Hoạt động xã hội

_20 từ/cụm từ về tụ tập, đi chơi, tổ chức tiệc và những hoạt động giải trí cùng bạn bè._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **hang out** | /ˌhæŋ ˈaʊt/ | phr v | đi chơi, tụ tập | to spend time with friends informally | We often hang out at the park. |
| 2 | **meet up** | /ˌmiːt ˈʌp/ | phr v | gặp nhau | to meet someone by arrangement | Let's meet up after work. |
| 3 | **night out** | /ˌnaɪt ˈaʊt/ | n | buổi tối đi chơi | an evening spent enjoying yourself | We had a great night out in town. |
| 4 | **throw a party** | /ˌθrəʊ ə ˈpɑːti/ | phrase | tổ chức tiệc | to organise a party | They threw a party for their anniversary. |

| 7 | **coffee shop** | /ˈkɒfi ʃɒp/ | n | quán cà phê | a place that serves coffee and light meals | We met at a small coffee shop near the station. |
| 8 | **theatre** | /ˈθɪətə(r)/ | n | rạp hát, nhà hát | a building where plays and shows are performed | Let's go to the theatre on Friday. |
| 9 | **bowling** | /ˈbəʊlɪŋ/ | n | trò chơi bowling | a game in which you roll a ball at pins | We went bowling for my birthday. |
| 10 | **dancing** | /ˈdɑːnsɪŋ/ | n | khiêu vũ | moving your body to music | There was dancing after dinner. |
| 11 | **clubbing** | /ˈklʌbɪŋ/ | n | đi hộp đêm | going to nightclubs | He enjoys clubbing with his friends. |
| 12 | **gig** | /ɡɪɡ/ | n | buổi biểu diễn nhạc sống | a live music performance | We saw a great gig at a small venue. |
| 13 | **sleepover** | /ˈsliːpəʊvə(r)/ | n | buổi ngủ qua đêm | a night when friends stay at your house | My daughter is having a sleepover tonight. |
| 14 | **game night** | /ˈɡeɪm naɪt/ | n | đêm chơi trò chơi | an evening spent playing games | Sunday is our family game night. |
| 15 | **outing** | /ˈaʊtɪŋ/ | n | chuyến đi chơi ngắn | a short trip taken for pleasure | The school organised an outing to the museum. |
| 16 | **get-together** | /ˈɡet təɡeðə(r)/ | n | buổi gặp mặt | an informal social meeting | We had a small get-together at home. |
| 17 | **socialise** | /ˈsəʊʃəlaɪz/ | v | giao lưu, giao tiếp xã hội | to spend time with other people | I like to socialise with my colleagues. |
| 18 | **mingle** | /ˈmɪŋɡl/ | v | hoà mình vào đám đông | to move around and talk to many people | She mingled with the guests at the party. |
| 19 | **RSVP** | /ˌɑːr es viː ˈpiː/ | abbr | xin xác nhận tham dự | please reply to an invitation | Please RSVP by Friday. |
| 20 | **venue** | /ˈvenjuː/ | n | địa điểm tổ chức | the place where an event happens | The venue can hold two hundred people. |

### Bài 3. Giữ liên lạc & trò chuyện

_20 từ/cụm từ về nhắn tin, gọi điện, mạng xã hội và cách duy trì liên lạc với bạn bè._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **keep in touch** | /ˌkiːp ɪn ˈtʌtʃ/ | phrase | giữ liên lạc | to stay in contact with someone | We keep in touch through social media. |
| 2 | **text** | /tekst/ | v/n | nhắn tin; tin nhắn | to send a written message by phone | I texted her to say I was late. |
| 3 | **message** | /ˈmesɪdʒ/ | n/v | tin nhắn; nhắn tin | a written or spoken piece of information | She sent me a long message. |
| 4 | **call back** | /ˌkɔːl ˈbæk/ | phr v | gọi lại | to phone someone again | I'll call you back after the meeting. |
| 5 | **voicemail** | /ˈvɔɪsmeɪl/ | n | thư thoại | a recorded phone message | I left him a voicemail. |
| 6 | **email** | /ˈiːmeɪl/ | n/v | thư điện tử; gửi email | a message sent by computer | Please email me the details. |
| 7 | **social media** | /ˌsəʊʃl ˈmiːdiə/ | n | mạng xã hội | websites where people share content | She spends too much time on social media. |
| 8 | **reply** | /rɪˈplaɪ/ | v/n | trả lời | to answer someone | He replied to my message quickly. |
| 9 | **forward** | /ˈfɔːwəd/ | v | chuyển tiếp | to send a message to someone else | Can you forward me that email? |
| 10 | **attachment** | /əˈtætʃmənt/ | n | tệp đính kèm | a file sent with an email | I opened the attachment but it was empty. |
| 11 | **group chat** | /ˈɡruːp tʃæt/ | n | nhóm trò chuyện | a conversation between several people online | Our group chat gets very busy at weekends. |
| 12 | **video call** | /ˈvɪdiəʊ kɔːl/ | n | cuộc gọi video | a phone call with pictures | We had a video call with our cousins abroad. |
| 13 | **face-to-face** | /ˌfeɪs tə ˈfeɪs/ | adj/adv | trực tiếp, mặt đối mặt | in person, not online | We finally met face-to-face. |
| 14 | **body language** | /ˈbɒdi læŋɡwɪdʒ/ | n | ngôn ngữ cơ thể | the way you move your body to show feelings | Her body language showed that she was nervous. |
| 15 | **conversation** | /ˌkɒnvəˈseɪʃn/ | n | cuộc trò chuyện | a talk between people | We had a long conversation about our plans. |
| 16 | **topic** | /ˈtɒpɪk/ | n | chủ đề | the subject of a conversation | We changed the topic when she arrived. |
| 17 | **rumour** | /ˈruːmə(r)/ | n | tin đồn | information that may not be true | There is a rumour that they are moving away. |
| 18 | **update** | /ˌʌpˈdeɪt/ | n/v | thông tin mới; cập nhật | new information; to give the latest news | She gave me an update on her new job. |
| 19 | **contact** | /ˈkɒntækt/ | n/v | liên lạc | communication with someone | I lost contact with him after school. |
| 20 | **reach** | /riːtʃ/ | v | liên lạc được với | to be able to contact someone | You can reach me on my mobile. |

### Bài 4. Bất đồng & làm hoà

_20 từ/cụm từ về tranh cãi, hiểu lầm, xin lỗi, tha thứ và cách giải quyết mâu thuẫn._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **argument** | /ˈɑːɡjumənt/ | n | cuộc tranh cãi | an angry disagreement | They had an argument about the bill. |
| 2 | **disagreement** | /ˌdɪsəˈɡriːmənt/ | n | sự bất đồng | a situation where people have different opinions | We had a disagreement but stayed friends. |
| 3 | **misunderstanding** | /ˌmɪsʌndəˈstændɪŋ/ | n | sự hiểu lầm | a failure to understand each other | The mistake was caused by a misunderstanding. |
| 4 | **quarrel** | /ˈkwɒrəl/ | n/v | cuộc cãi vã; cãi vã | an angry argument, often about something small | The sisters had a quarrel over a dress. |
| 5 | **fight** | /faɪt/ | n/v | cuộc đánh nhau; tranh đấu | a physical or serious argument | They had a fight at school. |
| 6 | **apologise** | /əˈpɒlədʒaɪz/ | v | xin lỗi | to say sorry | He apologised for his rude comment. |
| 7 | **apology** | /əˈpɒlədʒi/ | n | lời xin lỗi | a statement saying sorry | She accepted his apology. |
| 8 | **forgive** | /fəˈɡɪv/ | v | tha thứ | to stop being angry with someone | I can forgive a mistake, but not a lie. |
| 9 | **blame** | /bleɪm/ | v/n | đổ lỗi; sự đổ lỗi | to say someone is responsible for a problem | Don't blame me for the delay. |
| 10 | **accuse** | /əˈkjuːz/ | v | buộc tội, cáo buộc | to say someone has done something wrong | She accused him of lying. |
| 11 | **criticise** | /ˈkrɪtɪsaɪz/ | v | phê bình, chỉ trích | to say what is wrong with something | He criticises everything I do. |
| 12 | **insult** | /ɪnˈsʌlt/ | v/n | xúc phạm; lời xúc phạm | to say something rude to someone | She insulted him in front of everyone. |
| 13 | **offence** | /əˈfens/ | n | sự xúc phạm | something that makes someone upset | He took offence at my joke. |
| 14 | **take offence** | /ˌteɪk əˈfens/ | phrase | thấy bị xúc phạm | to feel upset by something someone says | She takes offence very easily. |
| 15 | **calm down** | /ˌkɑːm ˈdaʊn/ | phr v | bình tĩnh lại | to become less angry or excited | Take a deep breath and calm down. |
| 16 | **sort out** | /ˌsɔːt ˈaʊt/ | phr v | giải quyết | to solve a problem | We sorted out our differences. |
| 17 | **compromise** | /ˈkɒmprəmaɪz/ | n/v | sự thoả hiệp; thoả hiệp | an agreement where both sides give up something | Both sides had to compromise. |
| 18 | **trust** | /trʌst/ | n/v | sự tin tưởng; tin tưởng | a belief that someone is honest | Trust is important in any friendship. |
| 19 | **betray** | /bɪˈtreɪ/ | v | phản bội | to break someone's trust | She felt betrayed by her best friend. |
| 20 | **make peace** | /ˌmeɪk ˈpiːs/ | phrase | làm hoà | to end an argument | They finally made peace after a long talk. |

## Nhà cửa & nơi ở

`nha-cua-noi-o` · Từ vựng về loại nhà, đồ đạc, thuê nhà, chuyển nhà và những sự cố thường gặp trong nhà.

### Bài 1. Loại nhà & khu vực sống

_20 từ/cụm từ về căn hộ, nhà phố, biệt thự, tòa nhà và các tầng trong một ngôi nhà._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **accommodation** | /əˌkɒməˈdeɪʃn/ | n | chỗ ở | a place to live or stay | The price includes accommodation. |
| 2 | **apartment** | /əˈpɑːtmənt/ | n | căn hộ | a set of rooms in a larger building | They bought a two-bedroom apartment. |
| 3 | **bungalow** | /ˈbʌŋɡələʊ/ | n | nhà một tầng | a house with only one floor | My grandparents live in a bungalow. |
| 4 | **cottage** | /ˈkɒtɪdʒ/ | n | nhà nhỏ ở nông thôn | a small house in the countryside | We stayed in a charming cottage. |
| 5 | **villa** | /ˈvɪlə/ | n | biệt thự | a large comfortable house, often with a garden | The villa has a swimming pool. |
| 6 | **mansion** | /ˈmænʃn/ | n | dinh thự lớn | a very large impressive house | The film star lives in a huge mansion. |
| 7 | **studio flat** | /ˈstjuːdiəʊ flæt/ | n | căn hộ một phòng | a small flat with one main room | A studio flat is perfect for one person. |
| 8 | **dormitory** | /ˈdɔːmətri/ | n | ký túc xá | a building where students sleep | She lives in a university dormitory. |
| 9 | **hostel** | /ˈhɒstl/ | n | nhà nghỉ tập thể | a cheap place to stay with shared rooms | We booked a hostel near the beach. |
| 10 | **skyscraper** | /ˈskaɪskreɪpə(r)/ | n | tòa nhà chọc trời | a very tall building | The city is full of skyscrapers. |
| 11 | **building** | /ˈbɪldɪŋ/ | n | tòa nhà | a structure with a roof and walls | Our office is in that building. |
| 12 | **block of flats** | /ˌblɒk əv ˈflæts/ | n | khu chung cư | a building divided into several flats | They live on the fifth floor of a block of flats. |
| 13 | **ground floor** | /ˌɡraʊnd ˈflɔː(r)/ | n | tầng trệt | the floor at street level | The shop is on the ground floor. |
| 14 | **basement** | /ˈbeɪsmənt/ | n | tầng hầm | a room below ground level | We store old furniture in the basement. |
| 15 | **attic** | /ˈætɪk/ | n | gác mái | a room under the roof of a house | The children sleep in the attic. |
| 16 | **balcony** | /ˈbælkəni/ | n | ban công | a platform outside an upper window | We have breakfast on the balcony. |
| 17 | **terrace** | /ˈterəs/ | n | sân thượng, hiên | an outdoor area next to a building | The flat has a small roof terrace. |
| 18 | **garage** | /ˈɡærɑːʒ/ | n | ga-ra | a building for a car | The garage is next to the kitchen. |
| 19 | **garden** | /ˈɡɑːdn/ | n | khu vườn | an area with grass and plants outside a house | There is a large garden behind the house. |
| 20 | **yard** | /jɑːd/ | n | sân | an enclosed area outside a building | The children play in the yard. |

### Bài 2. Đồ đạc trong nhà

_20 từ/cụm từ về nội thất, đồ dùng phòng ngủ, phòng khách và thiết bị trong nhà._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **furniture** | /ˈfɜːnɪtʃə(r)/ | n | đồ nội thất | tables, chairs, beds and similar items | The flat came with all the furniture. |
| 2 | **cupboard** | /ˈkʌbəd/ | n | tủ đựng đồ | a piece of furniture with doors and shelves | The cups are in the kitchen cupboard. |

### Bài 3. Thuê nhà & chuyển nhà

_20 từ/cụm từ về hợp đồng thuê, tiền đặt cọc, chuyển nhà, hoá đơn và tìm nơi ở mới._

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 3 | **tenant** | /ˈtenənt/ | n | người thuê nhà | a person who rents a place | The tenant pays for electricity. |

| 10 | **pack** | /pæk/ | v | đóng gói, xếp đồ | to put things into boxes or bags | I need to pack all my books. |
| 11 | **unpack** | /ˌʌnˈpæk/ | v | mở đồ, dỡ hành lý | to take things out of boxes or bags | She unpacked her suitcase after the trip. |

| 17 | **suburb** | /ˈsʌbɜːb/ | n | vùng ngoại ô | an area outside the centre of a city | They live in a quiet suburb. |
| 18 | **location** | /ləʊˈkeɪʃn/ | n | vị trí | the place where something is | The flat is in a great location. |
| 19 | **furnished** | /ˈfɜːnɪʃt/ | adj | có đầy đủ nội thất | supplied with furniture | The apartment is fully furnished. |
| 20 | **unfurnished** | /ˌʌnˈfɜːnɪʃt/ | adj | không có nội thất | without furniture | We rented an unfurnished house and bought our own furniture. |

### Bài 4. Sự cố & hàng xóm

_20 từ/cụm từ về rò rỉ, ẩm mốc, sửa chữa, an ninh và cách ứng xử với hàng xóm._

| 8 | **lock** | /lɒk/ | n/v | ổ khoá; khoá | a device that keeps a door closed; to fasten it | Don't forget to lock the front door. |
| 9 | **key** | /kiː/ | n | chìa khoá | a small metal object used to open a lock | I left my keys at the office. |
| 10 | **burglar** | /ˈbɜːɡlə(r)/ | n | kẻ trộm đột nhập | a person who enters a building to steal | A burglar broke in through the window. |
| 11 | **burglary** | /ˈbɜːɡləri/ | n | vụ trộm đột nhập | the crime of entering a building to steal | There was a burglary in our street. |
| 12 | **alarm system** | /əˈlɑːm sɪstəm/ | n | hệ thống báo động | equipment that warns of danger or intruders | They installed an alarm system. |
| 13 | **noisy** | /ˈnɔɪzi/ | adj | ồn ào | making a lot of noise | The neighbours are noisy at weekends. |
| 14 | **noise** | /nɔɪz/ | n | tiếng ồn | a loud or unpleasant sound | The noise from the street kept me awake. |
| 15 | **complain** | /kəmˈpleɪn/ | v | phàn nàn | to say that you are unhappy about something | We complained about the noise. |
| 16 | **complaint** | /kəmˈpleɪnt/ | n | lời phàn nàn | a statement that something is wrong | The landlord ignored my complaint. |
| 17 | **community** | /kəˈmjuːnəti/ | n | cộng đồng | the people living in the same area | Everyone in the community helped clean the park. |
| 18 | **privacy** | /ˈprɪvəsi/ | n | sự riêng tư | the state of being free from public attention | The garden gives the house more privacy. |
| 19 | **power cut** | /ˈpaʊə kʌt/ | n | sự mất điện | a period when electricity is not supplied | The storm caused a power cut. |
| 20 | **maintenance** | /ˈmeɪntənəns/ | n | việc bảo trì | the work of keeping something in good condition | The building needs regular maintenance. |

## Trường học & học tập

`truong-hoc-hoc-tap` · Từ vựng về cấp học, môn học, hoạt động học tập, kỳ thi và đời sống học đường.

### Bài 1. Cấp học & nơi học

_20 từ/cụm từ về các cấp học, học kỳ, môn học, giảng đường và khuôn viên trường._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **education** | /ˌedʒuˈkeɪʃn/ | n | nền giáo dục | the process of teaching and learning | Every child should have access to education. |
| 2 | **primary school** | /ˈpraɪməri skuːl/ | n | trường tiểu học | a school for children aged about 5–11 | She teaches at a primary school. |
| 3 | **secondary school** | /ˈsekəndri skuːl/ | n | trường trung học | a school for children aged about 11–18 | He started secondary school last year. |
| 4 | **high school** | /ˈhaɪ skuːl/ | n | trường trung học phổ thông | a school for older children, especially in the US | My cousin is in her final year of high school. |
| 5 | **academy** | /əˈkædəmi/ | n | học viện | a school for a special subject or skill | She studies music at an academy in London. |
| 6 | **college** | /ˈkɒlɪdʒ/ | n | trường cao đẳng | a place of higher or further education | He is taking a cooking course at college. |
| 7 | **kindergarten** | /ˈkɪndəɡɑːtn/ | n | trường mẫu giáo | a school for very young children | My son starts kindergarten in September. |
| 8 | **nursery** | /ˈnɜːsəri/ | n | nhà trẻ | a place where very young children are cared for | She works at a nursery near her home. |
| 9 | **term** | /tɜːm/ | n | học kỳ | one of the periods in a school year | We have exams at the end of term. |
| 10 | **semester** | /sɪˈmestə(r)/ | n | học kỳ (đại học) | one of two periods in an academic year | The first semester ends in January. |
| 11 | **academic year** | /ˌækəˈdemɪk jɪə(r)/ | n | năm học | the period when schools and universities teach | The academic year begins in autumn. |
| 12 | **curriculum** | /kəˈrɪkjələm/ | n | chương trình học | the subjects taught in a school or course | The school has updated its curriculum. |
| 13 | **subject** | /ˈsʌbdʒɪkt/ | n | môn học | an area of knowledge studied at school | Science is my favourite subject. |
| 14 | **course** | /kɔːs/ | n | khoá học | a series of lessons on one subject | I signed up for an online English course. |
| 15 | **lecture** | /ˈlektʃə(r)/ | n | bài giảng | a talk given to a large group of students | The lecture lasted two hours. |
| 16 | **seminar** | /ˈsemɪnɑː(r)/ | n | buổi thảo luận | a small class where students discuss a topic | We have a seminar every Thursday. |
| 17 | **campus** | /ˈkæmpəs/ | n | khuôn viên trường | the land and buildings of a university | The campus has a large sports centre. |
| 18 | **classroom** | /ˈklɑːsruːm/ | n | phòng học | a room where lessons take place | The classroom was bright and quiet. |
| 19 | **playground** | /ˈpleɪɡraʊnd/ | n | sân chơi | an outdoor area where children play | The children ran into the playground. |
| 20 | **tutor** | /ˈtjuːtə(r)/ | n | giáo viên phụ đạo | a teacher who works with one student or a small group | My tutor helped me with my essay. |

### Bài 2. Hoạt động học tập

_20 từ/cụm từ về ôn bài, ghi chú, nghiên cứu, viết bài, thuyết trình và nhận phản hồi._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **study** | /ˈstʌdi/ | v/n | học, nghiên cứu | to spend time learning about something | I study English every morning. |
| 2 | **revise** | /rɪˈvaɪz/ | v | ôn tập | to prepare for an exam by reviewing work | She is revising for her history test. |
| 3 | **review** | /rɪˈvjuː/ | v/n | xem lại, ôn lại; bài đánh giá | to look at something again; a careful examination | Review your notes before the class. |

| 7 | **essay** | /ˈeseɪ/ | n | bài luận | a short piece of writing on a subject | I have to write a 500-word essay. |
| 8 | **assignment** | /əˈsaɪnmənt/ | n | bài tập được giao | a piece of work given to a student | The assignment must be submitted online. |
| 9 | **project** | /ˈprɒdʒekt/ | n | dự án | a piece of work involving study and planning | Our group is working on a science project. |
| 10 | **presentation** | /ˌpreznˈteɪʃn/ | n | bài thuyết trình | a talk in which you show and explain something | I gave a presentation about my country. |
| 11 | **group work** | /ˈɡruːp wɜːk/ | n | làm việc nhóm | work done together with other students | The teacher encourages group work. |
| 12 | **experiment** | /ɪkˈsperɪmənt/ | n/v | thí nghiệm; làm thí nghiệm | a scientific test | We did an experiment in the chemistry lab. |
| 13 | **report** | /rɪˈpɔːt/ | n/v | báo cáo; báo cáo lại | a written or spoken account of something | She wrote a report on the survey. |
| 14 | **source** | /sɔːs/ | n | nguồn tài liệu | a person or document that provides information | Always check your sources online. |
| 15 | **summary** | /ˈsʌməri/ | n | bản tóm tắt | a short account of the main points | Write a summary of the article. |
| 16 | **outline** | /ˈaʊtlaɪn/ | n/v | dàn ý; phác thảo | a plan showing the main points | Make an outline before you start writing. |
| 17 | **draft** | /drɑːft/ | n/v | bản nháp; viết nháp | an early version of a piece of writing | This is only the first draft. |
| 18 | **feedback** | /ˈfiːdbæk/ | n | phản hồi | advice or comments about your work | The tutor gave me feedback on my essay. |
| 19 | **progress** | /ˈprəʊɡres/ | n | sự tiến bộ | improvement over time | You have made good progress this term. |
| 20 | **achieve** | /əˈtʃiːv/ | v | đạt được | to succeed in reaching a goal | She achieved excellent results. |

### Bài 3. Kỳ thi & kết quả

_20 từ/cụm từ về bài kiểm tra, điểm số, đỗ trượt, bằng cấp và lễ tốt nghiệp._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **test** | /test/ | n/v | bài kiểm tra; kiểm tra | a short examination; to examine something | We have a maths test tomorrow. |
| 2 | **quiz** | /kwɪz/ | n | bài kiểm tra nhanh | a short informal test | The teacher started the lesson with a quiz. |
| 3 | **score** | /skɔː(r)/ | n/v | điểm số; ghi điểm | the number of points you get; to get points | My score was higher than I expected. |
| 4 | **mark** | /mɑːk/ | n/v | điểm; chấm điểm | a letter or number showing quality; to grade | The teacher marked our essays. |
| 5 | **grade** | /ɡreɪd/ | n/v | điểm, cấp; chấm điểm | a level of quality or a mark | She got the highest grade in the class. |
| 6 | **pass** | /pɑːs/ | v | đỗ, vượt qua | to succeed in an exam | I passed my driving test first time. |
| 7 | **fail** | /feɪl/ | v | trượt, không đạt | to not succeed in an exam | He failed the exam and had to retake it. |
| 8 | **result** | /rɪˈzʌlt/ | n | kết quả | the outcome of an exam or action | The results will be published next week. |
| 9 | **certificate** | /səˈtɪfɪkət/ | n | chứng chỉ | an official document proving a qualification | She received a certificate after the course. |
| 10 | **degree** | /dɪˈɡriː/ | n | bằng đại học | a qualification from a university | He has a degree in economics. |
| 11 | **diploma** | /dɪˈpləʊmə/ | n | bằng tốt nghiệp | a qualification from a college or school | She earned a diploma in design. |
| 12 | **entrance exam** | /ˈentrəns ɪɡˌzæm/ | n | kỳ thi đầu vào | a test used to decide who can enter a school | The entrance exam is very competitive. |
| 13 | **final exam** | /ˌfaɪnl ɪɡˈzæm/ | n | kỳ thi cuối kỳ | the last important exam of a course | We are preparing for the final exam. |
| 14 | **oral exam** | /ˈɔːrəl ɪɡˌzæm/ | n | kỳ thi nói | an exam where you speak instead of writing | The oral exam lasts about fifteen minutes. |
| 15 | **written exam** | /ˈrɪtn ɪɡˌzæm/ | n | kỳ thi viết | an exam where you write your answers | The written exam has three parts. |
| 16 | **multiple choice** | /ˌmʌltɪpl ˈtʃɔɪs/ | n/adj | trắc nghiệm | a test where you choose from several answers | The test was mostly multiple choice. |
| 17 | **cheat** | /tʃiːt/ | v | gian lận | to act dishonestly in an exam or game | Students who cheat will lose marks. |
| 18 | **retake** | /ˌriːˈteɪk/ | v/n | thi lại | to take an exam again | She decided to retake the test. |
| 19 | **graduate** | /ˈɡrædʒueɪt/ | v/n | tốt nghiệp; người tốt nghiệp | to complete a course of study | He graduated from university in June. |
| 20 | **graduation** | /ˌɡrædʒuˈeɪʃn/ | n | lễ tốt nghiệp | a ceremony when students receive their qualifications | My parents came to my graduation. |

### Bài 4. Đời sống học đường

_20 từ/cụm từ về đi học, vắng mặt, nội quy, áp lực thi cử và hoạt động ngoại khoá._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 2 | **absent** | /ˈæbsənt/ | adj | vắng mặt | not present in class | She was absent from school yesterday. |

| 7 | **uniform** | /ˈjuːnɪfɔːm/ | n | đồng phục | special clothes worn by students or workers | We have to wear a uniform at school. |

| 9 | **detention** | /dɪˈtenʃn/ | n | hình phạt ở lại trường | a punishment where a student stays after school | He got detention for being late. |
| 10 | **bully** | /ˈbʊli/ | v/n | bắt nạt; kẻ bắt nạt | to hurt or frighten someone weaker | Some older students bully younger children. |
| 11 | **bullying** | /ˈbʊliɪŋ/ | n | nạn bắt nạt | repeated behaviour that hurts or frightens someone | The school has a zero-tolerance policy on bullying. |
| 12 | **pressure** | /ˈpreʃə(r)/ | n | áp lực | a feeling of stress because of expectations | Students are under a lot of pressure. |
| 13 | **concentration** | /ˌkɒnsnˈtreɪʃn/ | n | sự tập trung | the ability to focus your attention | Loud music makes concentration difficult. |
| 14 | **distraction** | /dɪˈstrækʃn/ | n | điều gây xao nhãng | something that takes your attention away | Her phone is a constant distraction. |
| 15 | **participate** | /pɑːˈtɪsɪpeɪt/ | v | tham gia | to take part in an activity | Everyone is encouraged to participate in class. |
| 16 | **extra-curricular** | /ˌekstrə kəˈrɪkjələ(r)/ | adj | ngoại khoá | outside the normal school lessons | She does a lot of extra-curricular activities. |
| 17 | **club** | /klʌb/ | n | câu lạc bộ | a group of people who share an activity | I joined the photography club. |
| 18 | **competition** | /ˌkɒmpəˈtɪʃn/ | n | cuộc thi | an event where people try to win | She won a writing competition. |
| 19 | **principal** | /ˈprɪnsəpl/ | n | hiệu trưởng | the head of a school | The principal gave a short speech. |
| 20 | **discipline** | /ˈdɪsəplɪn/ | n | kỷ luật | controlled behaviour and clear rules | The school is known for its strong discipline. |

## Công việc & nghề nghiệp

`cong-viec-nghe-nghiep` · Từ vựng về nghề nghiệp, ứng tuyển, môi trường làm việc và các hình thức nghỉ việc.

| 20 | **entrepreneur** | /ˌɒntrəprəˈnɜː(r)/ | n | doanh nhân khởi nghiệp | a person who starts and runs a business | The young entrepreneur opened her first shop. |

### Bài 2. Ứng tuyển & tuyển dụng

_20 từ/cụm từ về nộp đơn, phỏng vấn, hồ sơ, vị trí tuyển dụng và quy trình chọn người._

| 20 | **appraisal** | /əˈpreɪzl/ | n | buổi đánh giá nhân viên | a formal review of an employee's work | My appraisal is scheduled for Friday. |

### Bài 4. Nghỉ việc & quyền lợi

_20 từ/cụm từ về xin việc, bị sa thải, trợ cấp, lương thưởng và các hình thức làm việc._

| 10 | **pension** | /ˈpenʃn/ | n | lương hưu | money paid regularly after retirement | He lives on a small pension. |
| 11 | **notice** | /ˈnəʊtɪs/ | n | thông báo nghỉ việc | advance warning before leaving a job | She gave one month's notice. |

| 15 | **annual leave** | /ˌænjuəl ˈliːv/ | n | phép năm | paid holiday days from work | I have five days of annual leave left. |

| 18 | **part-time** | /ˌpɑːt ˈtaɪm/ | adj/adv | bán thời gian | working only part of the week | He has a part-time job in a bookshop. |
| 19 | **full-time** | /ˌfʊl ˈtaɪm/ | adj/adv | toàn thời gian | working the normal number of hours | She is looking for a full-time position. |

## Mua sắm & tiền bạc

`mua-sam-tien-bac` · Từ vựng về cửa hàng, thanh toán, lập ngân sách và xử lý các vấn đề khi mua hàng.

### Bài 1. Cửa hàng & mua sắm

_20 từ/cụm từ về địa điểm mua sắm, quầy thanh toán, thử đồ và các bước khi đi mua hàng._

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 5 | **checkout** | /ˈtʃekaʊt/ | n | quầy thanh toán | the place where you pay in a shop | Please pay at the checkout. |
| 6 | **trolley** | /ˈtrɒli/ | n | xe đẩy hàng | a metal cart used in a supermarket | Our trolley was full of groceries. |
| 7 | **basket** | /ˈbɑːskɪt/ | n | giỏ hàng | a container used to carry shopping | I only need a basket for a few things. |
| 8 | **receipt** | /rɪˈsiːt/ | n | hoá đơn | a piece of paper proving you paid | Could I have a receipt, please? |
| 9 | **refund** | /ˈriːfʌnd/ | n/v | sự hoàn tiền; hoàn tiền | money returned for a product; to return money | I asked for a refund because the shirt was damaged. |
| 10 | **exchange** | /ɪksˈtʃeɪndʒ/ | v/n | đổi hàng; sự trao đổi | to give something back for another item | Can I exchange this jumper for a larger size? |
| 11 | **try on** | /ˌtraɪ ˈɒn/ | phr v | mặc thử, thử đồ | to put on clothes to see if they fit | May I try on this jacket? |
| 12 | **fitting room** | /ˈfɪtɪŋ ruːm/ | n | phòng thử đồ | a small room where you try on clothes | The fitting rooms are at the back. |

| 15 | **bargain** | /ˈbɑːɡən/ | n | món hời | something bought for less than its usual price | This coat was a real bargain. |
| 16 | **discount** | /ˈdɪskaʊnt/ | n | sự giảm giá | a reduction in the usual price | Students get a ten per cent discount. |
| 17 | **sale** | /seɪl/ | n | đợt giảm giá | a period when shops sell things cheaply | I bought these boots in the sale. |
| 18 | **price tag** | /ˈpraɪs tæɡ/ | n | nhãn giá | a label showing how much something costs | The price tag was still on the dress. |
| 19 | **purchase** | /ˈpɜːtʃəs/ | n/v | việc mua hàng; mua | something you buy; to buy something | Keep your receipt as proof of purchase. |
| 20 | **browse** | /braʊz/ | v | xem hàng, lướt qua | to look at things without planning to buy | I like to browse in bookshops. |

### Bài 2. Tiền & thanh toán

### Bài 3. Chi tiêu & ngân sách

_20 từ/cụm từ về lập ngân sách, tiết kiệm, chi tiêu hợp lý và phân biệt nhu cầu với xa xỉ._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 6 | **save** | /seɪv/ | v | tiết kiệm | to keep money for later | I try to save some money every month. |
| 7 | **waste** | /weɪst/ | v/n | lãng phí | to use money or time badly | Don't waste money on things you don't need. |
| 8 | **worth** | /wɜːθ/ | adj | đáng giá | having a particular value | The tickets are worth the price. |
| 9 | **value** | /ˈvæljuː/ | n | giá trị | how much something is worth | The value of the house has increased. |
| 10 | **cost** | /kɒst/ | n/v | chi phí; tốn | the amount of money needed; to have a price | The cost of living is rising. |
| 11 | **splurge** | /splɜːdʒ/ | v | tiêu mạnh tay | to spend a lot of money on something special | We splurged on a nice dinner. |
| 12 | **overspend** | /ˌəʊvəˈspend/ | v | chi quá mức | to spend more than you planned | We overspent on holiday. |
| 13 | **save up** | /ˌseɪv ˈʌp/ | phr v | để dành tiền | to keep money until you have enough | She is saving up for a new laptop. |
| 14 | **pay off** | /ˌpeɪ ˈɒf/ | phr v | trả hết (nợ) | to finish paying a debt | It took five years to pay off the loan. |
| 15 | **essential** | /ɪˈsenʃl/ | adj/n | thiết yếu | completely necessary | Water is essential for life. |
| 16 | **luxury** | /ˈlʌkʃəri/ | n | sự xa xỉ, đồ xa xỉ | something expensive that is not necessary | A holiday abroad is a luxury for us. |
| 17 | **second-hand** | /ˌsekənd ˈhænd/ | adj | đồ cũ | previously owned by someone else | I bought a second-hand bike. |
| 18 | **pocket money** | /ˈpɒkɪt mʌni/ | n | tiền tiêu vặt | small amount of money for daily spending | The children earn pocket money by helping at home. |
| 19 | **bank account** | /ˈbæŋk əkaʊnt/ | n | tài khoản ngân hàng | an account for keeping money at a bank | I opened a bank account when I was eighteen. |
| 20 | **interest** | /ˈɪntrəst/ | n | lãi suất; sự quan tâm | extra money paid on savings or loans | The account pays little interest. |

### Bài 4. Sự cố khi mua hàng

_20 từ/cụm từ về hàng lỗi, bảo hành, đổi trả, hàng giả, giao hàng và quyền lợi người tiêu dùng._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **faulty** | /ˈfɔːlti/ | adj | bị lỗi | not working correctly | The charger was faulty and didn't work. |
| 2 | **damaged** | /ˈdæmɪdʒd/ | adj | bị hư hại | broken or harmed | The box arrived damaged. |
| 3 | **defective** | /dɪˈfektɪv/ | adj | bị lỗi sản xuất | having a manufacturing fault | The shop replaced the defective product. |
| 4 | **warranty** | /ˈwɒrənti/ | n | bảo hành | a promise to repair a product for a period | The laptop has a two-year warranty. |
| 5 | **guarantee** | /ˌɡærənˈtiː/ | n/v | sự bảo đảm; bảo đảm | a promise that something will work | The watch comes with a money-back guarantee. |
| 6 | **return** | /rɪˈtɜːn/ | v/n | trả lại; sự trả lại | to give something back to the shop | You can return the item within thirty days. |
| 7 | **counterfeit** | /ˈkaʊntəfɪt/ | adj | giả mạo | made to look real in order to trick people | The market was full of counterfeit handbags. |
| 8 | **fake** | /feɪk/ | adj/n | giả; đồ giả | not real | I realised the ticket was fake. |
| 9 | **rip-off** | /ˈrɪp ɒf/ | n | giá cắt cổ | something that costs much more than it is worth | Twenty pounds for a coffee is a rip-off. |
| 10 | **overcharge** | /ˌəʊvəˈtʃɑːdʒ/ | v | tính giá quá cao | to ask for more money than is fair | The taxi driver tried to overcharge us. |
| 11 | **out of stock** | /ˌaʊt əv ˈstɒk/ | phrase | hết hàng | not available to buy at the moment | The shoes are out of stock in my size. |
| 12 | **in stock** | /ˌɪn ˈstɒk/ | phrase | còn hàng | available to buy | The book is in stock online. |
| 13 | **delivery** | /dɪˈlɪvəri/ | n | việc giao hàng | the act of bringing goods to a customer | Delivery usually takes three days. |
| 14 | **order** | /ˈɔːdə(r)/ | n/v | đơn hàng; đặt hàng | a request for goods; to ask for goods | I placed an order for two shirts. |
| 15 | **online shopping** | /ˌɒnlaɪn ˈʃɒpɪŋ/ | n | mua sắm trực tuyến | buying things on the internet | Online shopping is convenient but less personal. |
| 16 | **scam** | /skæm/ | n | vụ lừa đảo | dishonest scheme to take money | The email was a scam. |
| 17 | **consumer** | /kənˈsjuːmə(r)/ | n | người tiêu dùng | a person who buys goods or services | Consumers have the right to a refund. |
| 18 | **customer service** | /ˈkʌstəmə sɜːvɪs/ | n | dịch vụ khách hàng | help given to customers by a company | The customer service team answered quickly. |
| 19 | **replacement** | /rɪˈpleɪsmənt/ | n | hàng thay thế | a new item that replaces a faulty one | They sent a replacement at no extra cost. |
| 20 | **policy** | /ˈpɒləsi/ | n | chính sách | a set of rules for a company or organisation | Read the return policy before you buy. |

## Thực phẩm & nấu ăn

`thuc-pham-nau-an` · Từ vựng về nguyên liệu, cách nấu, khẩu vị và những tình huống khi ăn ngoài.

### Bài 1. Nguyên liệu & gia vị

_20 từ/cụm từ về nguyên liệu nấu ăn, gia vị, rau củ và các loại thịt thường gặp._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 3 | **flour** | /ˈflaʊə(r)/ | n | bột mì | powder made from grain, used in baking | Add two cups of flour to the bowl. |
| 4 | **sugar** | /ˈʃʊɡə(r)/ | n | đường | a sweet substance used in food and drinks | Do you take sugar in your tea? |
| 5 | **salt** | /sɔːlt/ | n | muối | a white substance used to flavour food | Add a pinch of salt. |
| 6 | **pepper** | /ˈpepə(r)/ | n | hạt tiêu | a hot spice used to flavour food | Season the soup with salt and pepper. |
| 7 | **oil** | /ɔɪl/ | n | dầu ăn | a thick liquid used for cooking | Heat some oil in the pan. |
| 8 | **butter** | /ˈbʌtə(r)/ | n | bơ | a soft yellow food made from cream | Spread some butter on the bread. |
| 9 | **cheese** | /tʃiːz/ | n | phô mai | a food made from milk | This pizza has too much cheese. |
| 10 | **garlic** | /ˈɡɑːlɪk/ | n | tỏi | a strong-smelling plant used in cooking | The sauce tastes strongly of garlic. |
| 11 | **onion** | /ˈʌnjən/ | n | hành | a round vegetable with a strong taste | Chop the onion finely. |
| 12 | **tomato** | /təˈmɑːtəʊ/ | n | cà chua | a soft red fruit used in salads and sauces | I put tomato in my sandwich. |
| 13 | **potato** | /pəˈteɪtəʊ/ | n | khoai tây | a round vegetable that grows underground | We had roast potato with dinner. |
| 14 | **carrot** | /ˈkærət/ | n | cà rốt | a long orange vegetable | Rabbits love eating carrots. |
| 15 | **beef** | /biːf/ | n | thịt bò | meat from a cow | The beef needs to cook for two hours. |
| 16 | **pork** | /pɔːk/ | n | thịt lợn | meat from a pig | She doesn't eat pork. |
| 17 | **lamb** | /læm/ | n | thịt cừu | meat from a young sheep | Lamb is popular at Easter. |
| 18 | **seafood** | /ˈsiːfuːd/ | n | hải sản | fish and shellfish eaten as food | The restaurant serves fresh seafood. |
| 19 | **sauce** | /sɔːs/ | n | nước sốt | a thick liquid served with food | Would you like some tomato sauce? |
| 20 | **spice** | /spaɪs/ | n | gia vị cay | a strong-flavoured substance used in cooking | This curry has a lot of spice. |

### Bài 2. Cách chế biến

_20 động từ chỉ cách nấu, cắt, trộn, nêm nếm và trình bày món ăn._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **boil** | /bɔɪl/ | v | luộc, đun sôi | to cook in boiling water | Boil the rice for twenty minutes. |
| 2 | **fry** | /fraɪ/ | v | chiên, rán | to cook in hot oil | Fry the onions until golden. |
| 3 | **bake** | /beɪk/ | v | nướng (bánh) | to cook in an oven | She baked a cake for his birthday. |
| 4 | **roast** | /rəʊst/ | v | nướng (thịt, rau) | to cook meat or vegetables in an oven | We roasted vegetables for dinner. |
| 5 | **grill** | /ɡrɪl/ | v | nướng trên vỉ | to cook over direct heat | Grill the fish for ten minutes. |
| 6 | **steam** | /stiːm/ | v/n | hấp; hơi nước | to cook with steam; hot water vapour | Steam the vegetables to keep them healthy. |
| 7 | **chop** | /tʃɒp/ | v | băm, chặt | to cut into small pieces | Chop the garlic finely. |
| 8 | **slice** | /slaɪs/ | v/n | thái lát; lát | to cut into thin pieces; a thin piece | Slice the bread and put it on a plate. |
| 9 | **peel** | /piːl/ | v/n | gọt vỏ; vỏ | to remove the skin from fruit or vegetables | Peel the potatoes before boiling them. |
| 10 | **mix** | /mɪks/ | v/n | trộn; hỗn hợp | to combine things together | Mix the flour and sugar in a bowl. |
| 11 | **stir** | /stɜː(r)/ | v | khuấy | to move something around with a spoon | Stir the soup slowly. |
| 12 | **pour** | /pɔː(r)/ | v | rót | to make liquid flow from a container | Pour the milk into the glass. |
| 13 | **add** | /æd/ | v | thêm | to put something with something else | Add a little salt to the sauce. |
| 14 | **taste** | /teɪst/ | v/n | nếm; vị | to try food; the flavour of food | Taste the soup before you serve it. |
| 15 | **serve** | /sɜːv/ | v | dọn ăn, phục vụ | to give food to people | Serve the dish with rice. |
| 16 | **heat** | /hiːt/ | v/n | làm nóng; sức nóng | to make something warm or hot | Heat the oil in a large pan. |
| 17 | **melt** | /melt/ | v | làm tan chảy | to change from solid to liquid | Melt the butter in a small pan. |
| 18 | **whisk** | /wɪsk/ | v | đánh trứng | to beat eggs or cream quickly | Whisk the eggs until they are light. |
| 19 | **garnish** | /ˈɡɑːnɪʃ/ | v/n | trang trí món ăn; đồ trang trí | to decorate food with a small amount of something | Garnish the soup with fresh herbs. |
| 20 | **prepare** | /prɪˈpeə(r)/ | v | chuẩn bị | to make food ready | I prepared dinner before they arrived. |

### Bài 3. Khẩu vị & bữa ăn

_20 từ/cụm từ về bữa ăn, khẩu phần, chế độ ăn và các vị cơ bản của món ăn._

| 8 | **diet** | /ˈdaɪət/ | n | chế độ ăn | the food you usually eat | A balanced diet is important for health. |
| 9 | **vegetarian** | /ˌvedʒəˈteəriən/ | n/adj | người ăn chay; ăn chay | a person who does not eat meat | The restaurant has many vegetarian dishes. |
| 10 | **vegan** | /ˈviːɡən/ | n/adj | người ăn thuần chay; thuần chay | a person who eats no animal products | She became vegan two years ago. |
| 11 | **hungry** | /ˈhʌŋɡri/ | adj | đói | wanting to eat | I was so hungry after the long walk. |
| 12 | **thirsty** | /ˈθɜːsti/ | adj | khát | wanting to drink | We were thirsty after the game. |
| 13 | **delicious** | /dɪˈlɪʃəs/ | adj | ngon | tasting very good | That cake was delicious. |

| 16 | **spicy** | /ˈspaɪsi/ | adj | cay | having a strong hot flavour | I love spicy food. |
| 17 | **sweet** | /swiːt/ | adj | ngọt | tasting like sugar | This tea is too sweet for me. |
| 18 | **sour** | /ˈsaʊə(r)/ | adj | chua | having a sharp taste like lemon | The milk has gone sour. |
| 19 | **salty** | /ˈsɔːlti/ | adj | mặn | containing too much salt | The chips were very salty. |
| 20 | **fresh** | /freʃ/ | adj | tươi | recently made, picked or caught | We bought fresh fish at the harbour. |

### Bài 4. Ăn ngoài & an toàn thực phẩm

_20 từ/cụm từ về đặt bàn, gọi món, hoá đơn, bảo quản thức ăn và dị ứng thực phẩm._

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 9 | **food poisoning** | /ˈfuːd pɔɪzənɪŋ/ | n | ngộ độc thực phẩm | illness caused by contaminated food | He got food poisoning from the seafood. |
| 10 | **hygiene** | /ˈhaɪdʒiːn/ | n | vệ sinh | the practice of keeping things clean | Food hygiene is closely checked. |
| 11 | **raw** | /rɔː/ | adj | sống, chưa nấu chín | not cooked | Do not eat raw chicken. |
| 12 | **undercooked** | /ˌʌndəˈkʊkt/ | adj | chưa chín kỹ | not cooked enough | The rice was slightly undercooked. |
| 13 | **contaminated** | /kənˈtæmɪneɪtɪd/ | adj | bị nhiễm khuẩn | made dirty or unsafe by bacteria | The water was contaminated. |
| 14 | **allergy** | /ˈælədʒi/ | n | dị ứng | a medical reaction to a substance | She has a serious nut allergy. |
| 15 | **allergic** | /əˈlɜːdʒɪk/ | adj | bị dị ứng | having an allergy | I am allergic to seafood. |
| 16 | **takeaway** | /ˈteɪkəweɪ/ | n | đồ ăn mang đi | food bought to eat somewhere else | We ordered a takeaway on Friday. |
| 17 | **service charge** | /ˈsɜːvɪs tʃɑːdʒ/ | n | phí phục vụ | an extra charge added to a restaurant bill | A service charge is included in the bill. |
| 18 | **cutlery** | /ˈkʌtləri/ | n | dao thìa dĩa | knives, forks and spoons | Could I have some clean cutlery? |
| 19 | **napkin** | /ˈnæpkɪn/ | n | khăn ăn | a piece of cloth or paper for wiping your mouth | She put a napkin on her lap. |
| 20 | **buffet** | /ˈbʊfeɪ/ | n | tiệc tự chọn | a meal where people serve themselves | The hotel offers a breakfast buffet. |

## Sức khỏe & thể chất

`suc-khoe-the-chat` · Từ vựng về bộ phận cơ thể, triệu chứng, điều trị và lối sống lành mạnh.

### Bài 1. Bộ phận cơ thể

_20 từ/cụm từ chỉ các bộ phận bên ngoài và cơ quan bên trong cơ thể người._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **forehead** | /ˈfɔːhed/ | n | trán | the front part of your head above your eyes | She touched his forehead to check his temperature. |
| 2 | **cheek** | /tʃiːk/ | n | má | the soft side of your face | The child had red cheeks. |
| 3 | **chin** | /tʃɪn/ | n | cằm | the part of your face below your mouth | He rested his chin on his hand. |
| 4 | **neck** | /nek/ | n | cổ | the part connecting your head to your body | My neck hurts after sitting at the computer. |
| 5 | **shoulder** | /ˈʃəʊldə(r)/ | n | vai | the part between your neck and arm | She carried the bag on her shoulder. |
| 6 | **elbow** | /ˈelbəʊ/ | n | khuỷu tay | the joint in the middle of your arm | He hit his elbow on the door. |
| 7 | **wrist** | /rɪst/ | n | cổ tay | the joint between your hand and arm | I wear a watch on my wrist. |
| 8 | **finger** | /ˈfɪŋɡə(r)/ | n | ngón tay | one of the long parts of your hand | She pointed her finger at the map. |
| 9 | **thumb** | /θʌm/ | n | ngón tay cái | the short thick finger on your hand | He hurt his thumb with the hammer. |
| 10 | **knee** | /niː/ | n | đầu gối | the joint in the middle of your leg | My knee hurts when I run. |
| 11 | **ankle** | /ˈæŋkl/ | n | mắt cá chân | the joint connecting your foot to your leg | She twisted her ankle on the stairs. |
| 12 | **back** | /bæk/ | n | lưng | the rear part of your body | He has a bad back. |
| 13 | **chest** | /tʃest/ | n | ngực | the front part of your body between neck and stomach | She felt a pain in her chest. |
| 14 | **stomach** | /ˈstʌmək/ | n | dạ dày, bụng | the organ where food is digested | My stomach is full. |
| 15 | **lung** | /lʌŋ/ | n | phổi | one of the organs used for breathing | Fresh air is good for your lungs. |
| 16 | **heart** | /hɑːt/ | n | tim | the organ that pumps blood | Exercise keeps your heart healthy. |
| 17 | **brain** | /breɪn/ | n | não | the organ that controls thought and movement | The brain needs rest to work well. |
| 18 | **skin** | /skɪn/ | n | da | the outer covering of your body | Protect your skin from the sun. |
| 19 | **bone** | /bəʊn/ | n | xương | one of the hard parts inside your body | Milk helps build strong bones. |
| 20 | **muscle** | /ˈmʌsl/ | n | cơ | tissue in the body that produces movement | Stretching helps prevent muscle pain. |

### Bài 2. Bệnh & chấn thương

_20 từ/cụm từ về bệnh thường gặp, triệu chứng, vết thương và cách mô tả cơn đau._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **illness** | /ˈɪlnəs/ | n | bệnh tật | the state of being unwell | She missed a week of work because of illness. |
| 2 | **disease** | /dɪˈziːz/ | n | căn bệnh | a medical condition affecting the body | Heart disease is a serious problem. |
| 3 | **flu** | /fluː/ | n | bệnh cúm | an illness like a bad cold, with fever | He is in bed with the flu. |
| 4 | **fever** | /ˈfiːvə(r)/ | n | sốt | a body temperature that is too high | The child has a high fever. |
| 5 | **cough** | /kɒf/ | n/v | ho | to push air out of your throat noisily | I have had a cough for a week. |
| 6 | **sneeze** | /sniːz/ | v/n | hắt hơi | to force air out of your nose suddenly | Cover your mouth when you sneeze. |
| 7 | **sore throat** | /ˌsɔː ˈθrəʊt/ | n | đau họng | a painful throat | Warm tea helps a sore throat. |
| 8 | **stomachache** | /ˈstʌməkeɪk/ | n | đau bụng | pain in your stomach | I had a stomachache after lunch. |
| 9 | **toothache** | /ˈtuːθeɪk/ | n | đau răng | pain in a tooth | He went to the dentist because of a toothache. |
| 10 | **earache** | /ˈɪəreɪk/ | n | đau tai | pain in your ear | The little boy has an earache. |
| 11 | **rash** | /ræʃ/ | n | phát ban | red spots on the skin | The rash disappeared after a few days. |
| 12 | **wound** | /wuːnd/ | n | vết thương | an injury that cuts or breaks the skin | The nurse cleaned the wound. |
| 13 | **injury** | /ˈɪndʒəri/ | n | chấn thương | damage to your body | He missed the final because of an injury. |
| 14 | **injure** | /ˈɪndʒə(r)/ | v | làm bị thương | to hurt someone or yourself | She injured her shoulder at the gym. |
| 15 | **sprain** | /spreɪn/ | v/n | bong gân | to damage a joint by twisting it | I sprained my ankle while running. |
| 16 | **fracture** | /ˈfræktʃə(r)/ | n/v | gãy xương | a break in a bone | The X-ray showed a small fracture. |
| 17 | **bleed** | /bliːd/ | v | chảy máu | to lose blood | His nose started to bleed. |
| 18 | **dizzy** | /ˈdɪzi/ | adj | chóng mặt | feeling that everything is spinning | I felt dizzy when I stood up. |
| 19 | **nausea** | /ˈnɔːziə/ | n | buồn nôn | the feeling that you might vomit | The medicine can cause nausea. |
| 20 | **symptom** | /ˈsɪmptəm/ | n | triệu chứng | a sign of an illness | A high temperature is a common symptom. |

### Bài 3. Khám & điều trị

_20 từ/cụm từ về đơn thuốc, thuốc, tiêm chủng, phẫu thuật và quá trình hồi phục._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 2 | **cure** | /kjʊə(r)/ | v/n | chữa khỏi; cách chữa | to make an illness go away completely | There is no cure for this virus. |

| 7 | **dose** | /dəʊs/ | n | liều lượng | the amount of medicine to take | Do not exceed the recommended dose. |

| 11 | **vaccine** | /ˈvæksiːn/ | n | vắc-xin | a substance that protects against a disease | The vaccine is free for children. |
| 12 | **injection** | /ɪnˈdʒekʃn/ | n | mũi tiêm | medicine put into the body with a needle | The nurse gave me an injection. |
| 13 | **bandage** | /ˈbændɪdʒ/ | n/v | băng gạc; băng bó | a strip of cloth used to cover a wound | The nurse put a bandage on his arm. |
| 14 | **plaster** | /ˈplɑːstə(r)/ | n | băng dán | a small sticky covering for a cut | I need a plaster for this cut. |
| 15 | **surgery** | /ˈsɜːdʒəri/ | n | phẫu thuật | medical treatment that involves cutting the body | He needed surgery on his back. |
| 16 | **operation** | /ˌɒpəˈreɪʃn/ | n | ca phẫu thuật | a medical procedure performed by a surgeon | The operation was successful. |
| 17 | **recover** | /rɪˈkʌvə(r)/ | v | hồi phục | to become well again | It took him a month to recover. |
| 18 | **recovery** | /rɪˈkʌvəri/ | n | sự hồi phục | the process of getting better | She made a full recovery. |
| 19 | **check-up** | /ˈtʃek ʌp/ | n | khám sức khỏe định kỳ | a regular medical examination | I have a dental check-up tomorrow. |
| 20 | **patient** | /ˈpeɪʃnt/ | n | bệnh nhân | a person receiving medical treatment | The doctor sees ten patients a day. |

| 12 | **sedentary** | /ˈsedntri/ | adj | ít vận động | spending a lot of time sitting down | Office workers often have a sedentary lifestyle. |

## Du lịch & kỳ nghỉ

`du-lich-ky-nghi` · Từ vựng về lên kế hoạch chuyến đi, sân bay, khách sạn, tham quan và xử lý sự cố khi du lịch.

### Bài 1. Lên kế hoạch chuyến đi

_20 từ/cụm từ về điểm đến, lịch trình, đặt vé, thủ tục và chuẩn bị cho chuyến đi._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **destination** | /ˌdestɪˈneɪʃn/ | n | điểm đến | the place you are travelling to | Paris is a popular destination for tourists. |
| 2 | **itinerary** | /aɪˈtɪnərəri/ | n | lịch trình chuyến đi | a plan of a journey | Our itinerary includes two days in Rome. |
| 3 | **travel agency** | /ˈtrævl eɪdʒənsi/ | n | đại lý du lịch | a company that arranges trips | We booked the tour through a travel agency. |
| 4 | **package holiday** | /ˈpækɪdʒ hɒlədeɪ/ | n | tour trọn gói | a holiday with travel and hotel included | A package holiday is easier to organise. |
| 5 | **booking** | /ˈbʊkɪŋ/ | n | việc đặt chỗ | an arrangement made in advance | I have a hotel booking for two nights. |
| 6 | **reserve** | /rɪˈzɜːv/ | v | đặt trước | to arrange to keep something for later | We reserved a table by the window. |
| 7 | **departure** | /dɪˈpɑːtʃə(r)/ | n | sự khởi hành | the act of leaving a place | The departure time is six in the morning. |
| 8 | **arrival** | /əˈraɪvl/ | n | sự đến nơi | the act of reaching a place | Our arrival was delayed by an hour. |
| 9 | **return ticket** | /rɪˈtɜːn tɪkɪt/ | n | vé khứ hồi | a ticket for a journey there and back | A return ticket is cheaper than two singles. |
| 10 | **one-way** | /ˌwʌn ˈweɪ/ | adj | một chiều | travelling in one direction only | I bought a one-way ticket to Berlin. |
| 11 | **round trip** | /ˌraʊnd ˈtrɪp/ | n | chuyến đi khứ hồi | a journey to a place and back | The round trip takes about four hours. |
| 12 | **check-in** | /ˈtʃek ɪn/ | n | thủ tục nhận phòng/lên máy bay | the process of registering at a hotel or airport | Check-in opens two hours before the flight. |
| 13 | **boarding pass** | /ˈbɔːdɪŋ pɑːs/ | n | thẻ lên máy bay | a document allowing you to board a plane | Show your boarding pass at the gate. |
| 14 | **gate** | /ɡeɪt/ | n | cổng lên máy bay | the place where you board a flight | Our flight leaves from gate twelve. |
| 15 | **customs** | /ˈkʌstəmz/ | n | hải quan | the place where goods are checked at a border | We went through customs quickly. |
| 16 | **visa** | /ˈviːzə/ | n | thị thực | an official permit to enter a country | You need a visa to visit that country. |
| 17 | **insurance** | /ɪnˈʃʊərəns/ | n | bảo hiểm | protection against loss or accidents | Travel insurance covers medical costs. |
| 18 | **souvenir** | /ˌsuːvəˈnɪə(r)/ | n | quà lưu niệm | something you buy to remember a place | I bought a small souvenir for my sister. |
| 19 | **guidebook** | /ˈɡaɪdbʊk/ | n | sách hướng dẫn du lịch | a book with information about a place | The guidebook recommends a quiet beach. |
| 20 | **abroad** | /əˈbrɔːd/ | adv | ở nước ngoài | in or to another country | She travels abroad every summer. |

### Bài 2. Ở sân bay & nhà ga

_20 từ/cụm từ về chuyến bay, thủ tục an ninh, hành lý, ghế ngồi và những trải nghiệm khi bay._

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **flight** | /flaɪt/ | n | chuyến bay | a journey by plane | Our flight was delayed by two hours. |
| 2 | **airline** | /ˈeəlaɪn/ | n | hãng hàng không | a company that operates flights | This airline offers cheap fares. |
| 3 | **take off** | /ˌteɪk ˈɒf/ | phr v | cất cánh | to leave the ground and start flying | The plane took off on time. |

| 11 | **departure lounge** | /dɪˈpɑːtʃə laʊndʒ/ | n | phòng chờ khởi hành | the area where passengers wait for flights | We waited in the departure lounge. |
| 12 | **terminal** | /ˈtɜːmɪnl/ | n | nhà ga sân bay | a building at an airport for passengers | The flight leaves from Terminal 2. |

| 14 | **seat belt** | /ˈsiːt belt/ | n | dây an toàn | a strap that keeps you safe in your seat | Please fasten your seat belt. |
| 15 | **aisle** | /aɪl/ | n | lối đi giữa các ghế | the narrow passage between seats | Could I have an aisle seat? |
| 16 | **window seat** | /ˈwɪndəʊ siːt/ | n | ghế cạnh cửa sổ | a seat next to the window | She chose a window seat to enjoy the view. |
| 17 | **connecting flight** | /kəˈnektɪŋ flaɪt/ | n | chuyến bay nối tiếp | a second flight that continues your journey | We missed our connecting flight. |
| 18 | **jet lag** | /ˈdʒet læɡ/ | n | sự mệt mỏi do lệch múi giờ | tiredness after flying across time zones | It took me days to get over jet lag. |
| 19 | **turbulence** | /ˈtɜːbjələns/ | n | sự rung lắc của máy bay | unsteady air that shakes a plane | The turbulence made me feel sick. |
| 20 | **runway** | /ˈrʌnweɪ/ | n | đường băng | the long strip where planes take off and land | The plane waited on the runway. |

_20 từ/cụm từ về mất đồ, trộm cắp, hỏng xe, chậm chuyến và cách nhờ trợ giúp khẩn cấp._

| 10 | **compensation** | /ˌkɒmpenˈseɪʃn/ | n | tiền bồi thường | money paid because of loss or damage | The airline offered us compensation. |
| 11 | **sunburn** | /ˈsʌnbɜːn/ | n | cháy nắng | red painful skin caused by the sun | She got bad sunburn on the first day. |

| 13 | **dehydrated** | /ˌdiːhaɪˈdreɪtɪd/ | adj | bị mất nước | having lost too much water from the body | I felt weak and dehydrated after the hike. |

| 18 | **traffic jam** | /ˈtræfɪk dʒæm/ | n | tắc đường | a long line of cars that cannot move | We were stuck in a traffic jam for an hour. |
