## Giao thông & di chuyển

`giao-thong-di-chuyen` · Từ vựng về phương tiện, đường sá, luật giao thông và đi lại bằng phương tiện công cộng.

### Bài 1. Phương tiện di chuyển

_20 từ/cụm từ về các loại xe, tàu, thuyền và phương tiện công cộng thường gặp._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **vehicle** | /ˈviːəkl/ | n | phương tiện | a machine used for transport | No vehicles are allowed in the old town. |
| 2 | **scooter** | /ˈskuːtə(r)/ | n | xe tay ga | a small motorbike or a child's vehicle | She rides a scooter to work. |
| 3 | **bicycle** | /ˈbaɪsɪkl/ | n | xe đạp | a vehicle with two wheels powered by pedals | I go to school by bicycle. |
| 4 | **coach** | /kəʊtʃ/ | n | xe khách đường dài | a comfortable bus for long journeys | We took a coach to Edinburgh. |
| 5 | **tram** | /træm/ | n | tàu điện | an electric vehicle that runs on rails in a city | The tram stops outside the market. |
| 6 | **subway** | /ˈsʌbweɪ/ | n | tàu điện ngầm (Mỹ) | an underground railway system | The subway is busy in the morning. |
| 7 | **underground** | /ˈʌndəɡraʊnd/ | n | tàu điện ngầm (Anh) | the London underground railway | Take the underground to the museum. |
| 8 | **ferry** | /ˈferi/ | n | phà | a boat that carries people across water | The ferry leaves every hour. |
| 9 | **boat** | /bəʊt/ | n | thuyền | a small vehicle for travelling on water | We rented a boat for the afternoon. |
| 10 | **ship** | /ʃɪp/ | n | tàu lớn | a large boat for sea travel | The ship crossed the ocean in five days. |
| 11 | **helicopter** | /ˈhelɪkɒptə(r)/ | n | trực thăng | an aircraft with rotating blades | A helicopter rescued the climbers. |
| 12 | **lorry** | /ˈlɒri/ | n | xe tải (Anh) | a large vehicle for carrying goods | A lorry blocked the road. |
| 13 | **truck** | /trʌk/ | n | xe tải (Mỹ) | a large vehicle for carrying goods | The truck delivered furniture to our house. |
| 14 | **van** | /væn/ | n | xe tải nhỏ | a covered vehicle for carrying goods | They moved the boxes in a van. |
| 15 | **carriage** | /ˈkærɪdʒ/ | n | toa tàu | one of the separate parts of a train | We sat in the last carriage. |
| 16 | **cable car** | /ˈkeɪbl kɑː(r)/ | n | cáp treo | a vehicle hanging from a moving cable | The cable car offers amazing views. |
| 17 | **minibus** | /ˈmɪnibʌs/ | n | xe buýt nhỏ | a small bus for about twelve people | A minibus took us to the hotel. |
| 18 | **ambulance** | /ˈæmbjələns/ | n | xe cứu thương | a vehicle that takes sick people to hospital | Someone called an ambulance. |
| 19 | **fire engine** | /ˈfaɪər endʒɪn/ | n | xe cứu hỏa | a vehicle used by firefighters | The fire engine arrived within minutes. |
| 20 | **caravan** | /ˈkærəvæn/ | n | xe nhà di động | a vehicle you can live in on holiday | We spent the summer in a caravan. |

| 11 | **tunnel** | /ˈtʌnl/ | n | đường hầm | a passage under the ground | The train went through a long tunnel. |
| 12 | **motorway** | /ˈməʊtəweɪ/ | n | đường cao tốc (Anh) | a wide road for fast traffic | The motorway was closed because of snow. |

| 19 | **path** | /pɑːθ/ | n | lối đi | a narrow way for walking | A path leads down to the beach. |
| 20 | **alley** | /ˈæli/ | n | ngõ hẹp | a narrow street between buildings | We found a small restaurant in an alley. |

## Thời tiết & môi trường

`thoi-tiet-moi-truong` · Từ vựng về thời tiết, khí hậu, thiên tai và những việc làm bảo vệ môi trường.

### Bài 1. Thời tiết hằng ngày

_20 từ/cụm từ về dự báo, nhiệt độ, nắng mưa, gió và các hiện tượng thời tiết thường gặp._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **forecast** | /ˈfɔːkɑːst/ | n/v | dự báo thời tiết; dự báo | a statement about future weather | The forecast looks good for the weekend. |
| 2 | **temperature** | /ˈtemprətʃə(r)/ | n | nhiệt độ | how hot or cold something is | The temperature dropped below zero. |
| 3 | **celsius** | /ˈselsiəs/ | n/adj | độ C | a scale for measuring temperature | It was thirty degrees celsius in the shade. |
| 4 | **bright** | /braɪt/ | adj | sáng sủa | full of light and sunshine | It was a bright, clear morning. |
| 5 | **overcast** | /ˌəʊvəˈkɑːst/ | adj | u ám, nhiều mây | covered with grey clouds | The sky was overcast before the storm. |
| 6 | **wet** | /wet/ | adj | ẩm ướt, có mưa | covered in water or raining | Take an umbrella — it's wet outside. |
| 7 | **gusty** | /ˈɡʌsti/ | adj | gió từng cơn mạnh | with strong sudden winds | It was too gusty to go sailing. |
| 8 | **foggy** | /ˈfɒɡi/ | adj | có sương mù | with thick cloud near the ground | The road was dangerous in foggy weather. |
| 9 | **stormy** | /ˈstɔːmi/ | adj | có bão | with strong wind and rain | We stayed indoors on stormy days. |
| 10 | **humid** | /ˈhjuːmɪd/ | adj | ẩm ướt (không khí) | having a lot of water in the air | Summer here is hot and humid. |
| 11 | **humidity** | /hjuːˈmɪdəti/ | n | độ ẩm | the amount of water in the air | The humidity makes it feel hotter. |
| 12 | **drizzle** | /ˈdrɪzl/ | n/v | mưa phùn; mưa lất phất | light rain | A fine drizzle continued all morning. |
| 13 | **downpour** | /ˈdaʊnpɔː(r)/ | n | mưa xối xả | a heavy sudden rain | We sheltered from the downpour under a tree. |
| 14 | **thunder** | /ˈθʌndə(r)/ | n | sấm | the loud noise during a storm | The thunder woke the children. |
| 15 | **lightning** | /ˈlaɪtnɪŋ/ | n | chớp | a sudden flash of light in a storm | Lightning struck a tree nearby. |
| 16 | **breeze** | /briːz/ | n | làn gió nhẹ | a gentle wind | A cool breeze came from the sea. |
| 17 | **frost** | /frɒst/ | n | sương giá | a thin layer of ice on surfaces | There was frost on the car this morning. |
| 18 | **heatwave** | /ˈhiːtweɪv/ | n | đợt nắng nóng | a long period of very hot weather | The heatwave lasted two weeks. |
| 19 | **chilly** | /ˈtʃɪli/ | adj | se lạnh | unpleasantly cold | It gets chilly in the evening. |
| 20 | **mild** | /maɪld/ | adj | ôn hòa | not very cold or severe | We had a mild winter this year. |

### Bài 2. Khí hậu & mùa

_20 từ/cụm từ về đới khí hậu, mùa mưa, mùa khô, lượng mưa và ánh sáng ban ngày._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **climate** | /ˈklaɪmət/ | n | khí hậu | the usual weather of a region | The island has a warm climate. |
| 2 | **tropical** | /ˈtrɒpɪkl/ | adj | nhiệt đới | from or relating to the hot regions near the equator | Tropical storms are common here. |
| 3 | **polar** | /ˈpəʊlə(r)/ | adj | hàn đới, ở hai cực | relating to the coldest areas of the earth | Polar ice is melting quickly. |
| 4 | **dry season** | /ˈdraɪ siːzn/ | n | mùa khô | the period of the year with little rain | Water is scarce during the dry season. |
| 5 | **wet season** | /ˈwet siːzn/ | n | mùa mưa | the period of the year with heavy rain | Roads flood in the wet season. |
| 6 | **monsoon** | /mɒnˈsuːn/ | n | gió mùa | a seasonal wind bringing heavy rain | The monsoon arrived early this year. |
| 7 | **equator** | /ɪˈkweɪtə(r)/ | n | đường xích đạo | the imaginary line around the middle of the earth | Countries near the equator are hot all year. |
| 8 | **hemisphere** | /ˈhemɪsfɪə(r)/ | n | bán cầu | half of the earth | Australia is in the southern hemisphere. |
| 9 | **weather pattern** | /ˈweðə pætn/ | n | kiểu thời tiết | a repeated way weather changes | Climate change is altering weather patterns. |
| 10 | **average** | /ˈævərɪdʒ/ | n/adj | mức trung bình; trung bình | the usual or middle amount | The average rainfall is high here. |
| 11 | **rainfall** | /ˈreɪnfɔːl/ | n | lượng mưa | the amount of rain that falls | Rainfall has decreased this year. |
| 12 | **sunshine** | /ˈsʌnʃaɪn/ | n | ánh nắng | light from the sun | We get plenty of sunshine in July. |
| 13 | **daylight** | /ˈdeɪlaɪt/ | n | ánh sáng ban ngày | natural light during the day | We finished the walk before daylight faded. |
| 14 | **sunrise** | /ˈsʌnraɪz/ | n | bình minh | the time when the sun comes up | We watched the sunrise from the beach. |
| 15 | **sunset** | /ˈsʌnset/ | n | hoàng hôn | the time when the sun goes down | The sunset was beautiful. |
| 16 | **climate zone** | /ˈklaɪmət zəʊn/ | n | đới khí hậu | an area with a particular climate | Vietnam has several climate zones. |
| 17 | **altitude** | /ˈæltɪtjuːd/ | n | độ cao | height above sea level | At this altitude, the air is thin. |
| 18 | **latitude** | /ˈlætɪtjuːd/ | n | vĩ độ | distance north or south of the equator | The latitude affects the length of the day. |
| 19 | **thermometer** | /θəˈmɒmɪtə(r)/ | n | nhiệt kế | an instrument for measuring temperature | The thermometer showed thirty degrees. |
| 20 | **seasonal** | /ˈsiːzənl/ | adj | theo mùa | happening at a particular time of year | Seasonal work is common in tourism. |

### Bài 3. Thiên tai

_20 từ/cụm từ về động đất, bão, lũ lụt, cháy rừng và công tác cứu hộ._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **disaster** | /dɪˈzɑːstə(r)/ | n | thảm họa | a very bad event causing great damage | The flood was the worst disaster in years. |
| 2 | **earthquake** | /ˈɜːθkweɪk/ | n | động đất | a sudden shaking of the ground | The earthquake measured six on the Richter scale. |
| 3 | **drought** | /draʊt/ | n | hạn hán | a long period without rain | The drought ruined the crops. |

| 5 | **typhoon** | /taɪˈfuːn/ | n | bão nhiệt đới | a violent tropical storm in the western Pacific | The typhoon forced schools to close. |
| 6 | **tornado** | /tɔːˈneɪdəʊ/ | n | lốc xoáy | a very strong spinning wind | A tornado damaged several houses. |
| 7 | **volcano** | /vɒlˈkeɪnəʊ/ | n | núi lửa | a mountain that can erupt | The volcano has been quiet for decades. |
| 8 | **eruption** | /ɪˈrʌpʃn/ | n | vụ phun trào | the sudden explosion of a volcano | The eruption sent ash into the sky. |
| 9 | **tsunami** | /tsuːˈnɑːmi/ | n | sóng thần | a huge wave caused by an earthquake | The tsunami warning was issued immediately. |
| 10 | **landslide** | /ˈlændslaɪd/ | n | sạt lở đất | a mass of earth falling down a hill | Heavy rain caused a landslide. |
| 11 | **avalanche** | /ˈævəlɑːnʃ/ | n | lở tuyết | a large amount of snow falling down a mountain | Two climbers were caught in an avalanche. |
| 12 | **wildfire** | /ˈwaɪldfaɪə(r)/ | n | cháy rừng | a fire that spreads quickly through countryside | The wildfire burned for a week. |
| 13 | **storm** | /stɔːm/ | n | cơn bão | very bad weather with strong wind and rain | A storm is expected tonight. |
| 14 | **blizzard** | /ˈblɪzəd/ | n | bão tuyết | a severe snowstorm with strong wind | The blizzard closed the mountain road. |
| 15 | **hail** | /heɪl/ | n | mưa đá | small balls of ice falling like rain | The hail damaged the car. |
| 16 | **victim** | /ˈvɪktɪm/ | n | nạn nhân | a person harmed by an event | The victims were given temporary housing. |
| 17 | **evacuate** | /ɪˈvækjueɪt/ | v | sơ tán | to move people away from danger | Residents were evacuated before the storm. |
| 18 | **shelter** | /ˈʃeltə(r)/ | n/v | nơi trú ẩn; trú ẩn | a safe place; to protect from danger | We took shelter in a school. |
| 19 | **rescue** | /ˈreskjuː/ | v/n | cứu hộ; cuộc cứu hộ | to save someone from danger | The team rescued three climbers. |
| 20 | **destroy** | /dɪˈstrɔɪ/ | v | phá hủy | to damage something completely | The fire destroyed the whole village. |

### Bài 4. Bảo vệ môi trường

_20 từ/cụm từ về ô nhiễm, rác thải, năng lượng tái tạo và hành động sống xanh._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **environment** | /ɪnˈvaɪrənmənt/ | n | môi trường | the natural world around us | We must protect the environment. |
| 2 | **pollution** | /pəˈluːʃn/ | n | sự ô nhiễm | damage to air, water or land | The city suffers from heavy air pollution. |
| 3 | **pollute** | /pəˈluːt/ | v | làm ô nhiễm | to make air, water or land dirty | Factories pollute the river. |
| 4 | **plastic waste** | /ˈplæstɪk weɪst/ | n | rác thải nhựa | discarded plastic materials | Plastic waste harms sea animals. |
| 5 | **litter** | /ˈlɪtə(r)/ | n | rác vứt bừa bãi | small pieces of rubbish left in public places | There was litter all over the beach. |
| 6 | **carbon footprint** | /ˈkɑːbən fʊtprɪnt/ | n | dấu chân carbon | the amount of carbon dioxide produced by an activity | Flying increases your carbon footprint. |
| 7 | **greenhouse gas** | /ˈɡriːnhaʊs ɡæs/ | n | khí nhà kính | a gas that traps heat in the atmosphere | Cars produce greenhouse gases. |
| 8 | **emission** | /ɪˈmɪʃn/ | n | khí thải | gas or other substance released into the air | The new law limits emissions. |
| 9 | **renewable energy** | /rɪˈnjuːəbl enədʒi/ | n | năng lượng tái tạo | energy from sources that do not run out | The country is investing in renewable energy. |
| 10 | **solar power** | /ˈsəʊlə paʊə(r)/ | n | năng lượng mặt trời | energy produced from sunlight | Solar power heats the water. |
| 11 | **wind power** | /ˈwɪnd paʊə(r)/ | n | năng lượng gió | energy produced from the wind | Wind power is common in coastal areas. |
| 12 | **fossil fuel** | /ˈfɒsl fjuːəl/ | n | nhiên liệu hóa thạch | coal, oil or gas formed underground | Burning fossil fuels causes climate change. |
| 13 | **deforestation** | /ˌdiːfɒrɪˈsteɪʃn/ | n | nạn phá rừng | the cutting down of large areas of forest | Deforestation threatens wildlife. |
| 14 | **endangered** | /ɪnˈdeɪndʒəd/ | adj | có nguy cơ tuyệt chủng | at risk of disappearing | The tiger is an endangered species. |
| 15 | **conservation** | /ˌkɒnsəˈveɪʃn/ | n | sự bảo tồn | the protection of nature | The park is a conservation area. |
| 16 | **sustainable** | /səˈsteɪnəbl/ | adj | bền vững | able to continue without harming the environment | We need more sustainable farming. |
| 17 | **eco-friendly** | /ˌiːkəʊ ˈfrendli/ | adj | thân thiện môi trường | not harmful to the environment | This packaging is eco-friendly. |
| 18 | **climate change** | /ˈklaɪmət tʃeɪndʒ/ | n | biến đổi khí hậu | long-term changes in the earth's climate | Climate change is affecting every country. |
| 19 | **sea level** | /ˈsiː levl/ | n | mực nước biển | the average height of the sea | Rising sea levels threaten coastal cities. |
| 20 | **biodegradable** | /ˌbaɪəʊdɪˈɡreɪdəbl/ | adj | phân hủy sinh học | able to decay naturally safely | Use biodegradable bags when you shop. |

## Thể thao & giải trí

`the-thao-giai-tri` · Từ vựng về môn thể thao, dụng cụ, thi đấu, giải thưởng và các chương trình giải trí.

### Bài 1. Các môn thể thao

| 18 | **climbing** | /ˈklaɪmɪŋ/ | n | leo núi | the sport of going up mountains or rocks | Climbing requires strength and skill. |

| 20 | **marathon** | /ˈmærəθən/ | n | cuộc chạy marathon | a running race of about 42 kilometres | She completed the marathon in four hours. |

| 19 | **volley** | /ˈvɒli/ | v/n | đá bóng nửa nảy; cú đá nửa nảy | to kick or hit a ball before it touches the ground | She volleyed the ball into the net. |
| 20 | **dribble** | /ˈdrɪbl/ | v | rê bóng | to move the ball forward with small touches | He dribbled past two defenders. |

| 20 | **athlete** | /ˈæθliːt/ | n | vận động viên | a person who is good at sports | The athletes trained for months. |

## Công nghệ & truyền thông

`cong-nghe-truyen-thong` · Từ vựng về thiết bị, internet, mạng xã hội, an toàn số và công nghệ hiện đại.

### Bài 1. Thiết bị công nghệ

| 6 | **mouse** | /maʊs/ | n | chuột máy tính | a device used to control a computer | Click the left button on the mouse. |
| 7 | **monitor** | /ˈmɒnɪtə(r)/ | n | màn hình | a screen for a computer | The monitor is too small for design work. |
| 8 | **printer** | /ˈprɪntə(r)/ | n | máy in | a machine that prints documents | The printer is out of paper. |

| 10 | **headphones** | /ˈhedfəʊnz/ | n | tai nghe | devices worn over the ears for sound | I listen to music with headphones. |
| 11 | **speaker** | /ˈspiːkə(r)/ | n | loa | a device that produces sound | The speaker is connected by Bluetooth. |
| 12 | **charger** | /ˈtʃɑːdʒə(r)/ | n | bộ sạc | a device used to charge a battery | I left my charger at the hotel. |
| 13 | **battery** | /ˈbætri/ | n | pin | a device that stores electrical power | My phone battery is almost dead. |
| 14 | **cable** | /ˈkeɪbl/ | n | dây cáp | a wire that connects devices | This cable is too short. |
| 15 | **USB** | /ˌjuː es ˈbiː/ | n | cổng USB | a standard connection for devices | Save the file on a USB drive. |
| 16 | **hard drive** | /ˈhɑːd draɪv/ | n | ổ cứng | a device for storing computer data | The hard drive is nearly full. |
| 17 | **webcam** | /ˈwebkæm/ | n | webcam | a camera connected to a computer | She turned on her webcam for the call. |
| 18 | **smartwatch** | /ˈsmɑːtwɒtʃ/ | n | đồng hồ thông minh | a watch with computer features | My smartwatch counts my steps. |
| 19 | **console** | /ˈkɒnsəʊl/ | n | máy chơi game | a device for playing video games | He bought a new games console. |
| 20 | **drone** | /drəʊn/ | n | máy bay không người lái | a small aircraft controlled remotely | The drone filmed the whole beach. |

### Bài 2. Internet & ứng dụng

_20 từ/cụm từ về trang web, tải xuống, tài khoản, mật khẩu và lưu trữ trực tuyến._

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 11 | **account** | /əˈkaʊnt/ | n | tài khoản | an arrangement allowing access to a service | Create an account to save your progress. |
| 12 | **password** | /ˈpɑːswɜːd/ | n | mật khẩu | a secret word used to access an account | Never share your password. |
| 13 | **username** | /ˈjuːzəneɪm/ | n | tên đăng nhập | the name you use on a website | My username is easy to remember. |
| 14 | **log in** | /ˌlɒɡ ˈɪn/ | phr v | đăng nhập | to enter an account with a password | Log in to see your messages. |

| 16 | **sign up** | /ˌsaɪn ˈʌp/ | phr v | đăng ký | to create a new account | Sign up for a free trial. |
| 17 | **profile** | /ˈprəʊfaɪl/ | n | hồ sơ cá nhân | the information about you on a website | She updated her profile picture. |
| 18 | **notification** | /ˌnəʊtɪfɪˈkeɪʃn/ | n | thông báo | a message alerting you about something | I turned off notifications at night. |
| 19 | **cloud storage** | /ˌklaʊd ˈstɔːrɪdʒ/ | n | lưu trữ đám mây | online space for saving files | I keep my photos in cloud storage. |
| 20 | **wifi** | /ˈwaɪfaɪ/ | n | wifi | a wireless internet connection | Do you know the wifi password? |

| 20 | **emoji** | /ɪˈməʊdʒi/ | n | biểu tượng cảm xúc | a small picture used in messages | She replied with a smiling emoji. |

## Thời trang & quần áo

`thoi-trang-quan-ao` · Từ vựng về trang phục, phụ kiện, chất liệu, cách giặt ủi và xu hướng thời trang.

| 18 | **earrings** | /ˈɪərɪŋz/ | n | khuyên tai | jewellery worn on the ears | She took off her earrings before bed. |
| 19 | **ring** | /rɪŋ/ | n | nhẫn | a circular piece of jewellery for a finger | He wore a silver ring on his little finger. |
| 20 | **sunglasses** | /ˈsʌnɡlɑːsɪz/ | n | kính râm | dark glasses that protect the eyes from the sun | Don't forget your sunglasses at the beach. |

_20 từ/cụm từ về kích cỡ, cảm giác khi mặc, phong cách ăn mặc và sửa quần áo._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |

| 20 | **button** | /ˈbʌtn/ | n | cúc áo | a small round object used to fasten clothes | A button fell off my coat. |

### Bài 3. Chất liệu & giặt ủi

| 12 | **shrink** | /ʃrɪŋk/ | v | co lại | to become smaller | Wool can shrink in hot water. |
| 13 | **fade** | /feɪd/ | v | phai màu | to lose colour | The curtains faded in the sun. |
| 14 | **detergent** | /dɪˈtɜːdʒənt/ | n | nước giặt | a liquid or powder used for washing clothes | Add a small amount of detergent. |
| 15 | **bleach** | /bliːtʃ/ | n/v | thuốc tẩy; tẩy trắng | a chemical used to make things white | Don't use bleach on coloured clothes. |

| 17 | **hanger** | /ˈhæŋə(r)/ | n | móc treo | a shaped object for hanging clothes | Put the dress on a hanger. |
| 18 | **dry-clean** | /ˌdraɪ ˈkliːn/ | v | giặt khô | to clean clothes with chemicals, not water | This suit must be dry-cleaned. |
| 19 | **mend** | /mend/ | v | vá | to repair a hole in clothing | She mended the hole in his sock. |
| 20 | **stitch** | /stɪtʃ/ | n/v | mũi khâu; khâu | a line of thread joining cloth | The seam needs a few stitches. |

## Cảm xúc & tính cách

| 12 | **satisfied** | /ˈsætɪsfaɪd/ | adj | hài lòng | pleased because something is good enough | The customer was satisfied with the service. |

| 20 | **glad** | /ɡlæd/ | adj | vui, hài lòng | pleased about something | I'm glad you could come. |

## Tình yêu & hôn nhân

`tinh-yeu-hon-nhan` · Từ vựng về hẹn hò, đính hôn, lễ cưới, đời sống vợ chồng và những cung bậc của tình yêu.

### Bài 1. Hẹn hò & thu hút

_20 từ/cụm từ về rung động, hẹn hò, bày tỏ tình cảm và chia tay._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **romance** | /rəʊˈmæns/ | n | sự lãng mạn, chuyện tình | a love affair or romantic feeling | Their romance began at university. |
| 2 | **romantic** | /rəʊˈmæntɪk/ | adj | lãng mạn | showing feelings of love | He planned a romantic dinner. |
| 3 | **crush** | /krʌʃ/ | n/v | say mê; đè bẹp | a strong but temporary liking for someone | She has a crush on a boy in her class. |

| 5 | **blind date** | /ˌblaɪnd ˈdeɪt/ | n | cuộc hẹn mù | a date arranged by other people | I was nervous about my blind date. |
| 6 | **appeal** | /əˈpiːl/ | n | sức hấp dẫn | a quality that makes someone attractive | There was an instant appeal between them. |
| 7 | **attracted** | /əˈtræktɪd/ | adj | bị thu hút | feeling interested or drawn to someone | He was attracted by her sense of humour. |
| 8 | **charm** | /tʃɑːm/ | n | sự quyến rũ | a pleasant quality that attracts people | She won everyone over with her charm. |
| 9 | **flirt** | /flɜːt/ | v | tán tỉnh | to behave playfully towards someone you like | He flirted with her all evening. |
| 10 | **adore** | /əˈdɔː(r)/ | v | yêu mến, say mê | to love someone very much | She adores her little nephew. |
| 11 | **fancy** | /ˈfænsi/ | v | thích (Anh-Anh) | to like or be attracted to someone | I think he fancies you. |
| 12 | **fall in love** | /ˌfɔːl ɪn ˈlʌv/ | phrase | phải lòng | to begin to love someone | They fell in love on holiday. |
| 13 | **ask out** | /ˌɑːsk ˈaʊt/ | phr v | mời đi hẹn | to invite someone on a date | He asked her out for a coffee. |
| 14 | **go out** | /ˌɡəʊ ˈaʊt/ | phr v | hẹn hò | to have a romantic relationship | They have been going out for a year. |
| 15 | **break up** | /ˌbreɪk ˈʌp/ | phr v | chia tay | to end a romantic relationship | They broke up after three years. |
| 16 | **split up** | /ˌsplɪt ˈʌp/ | phr v | chia tay | to separate from a partner | Her parents split up when she was young. |
| 17 | **ex** | /eks/ | n | người yêu cũ | a former husband, wife or partner | He is still friends with his ex. |
| 18 | **soulmate** | /ˈsəʊlmeɪt/ | n | tri kỷ | a person who understands you completely | She believes he is her soulmate. |
| 19 | **commitment** | /kəˈmɪtmənt/ | n | sự cam kết | a promise to stay with someone | He is afraid of commitment. |
| 20 | **faithful** | /ˈfeɪθfl/ | adj | chung thủy | loyal to a partner | She has always been faithful to him. |

### Bài 2. Đính hôn & lễ cưới

_20 từ/cụm từ về cầu hôn, lễ cưới, phù dâu phù rể và tuần trăng mật._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **engagement** | /ɪnˈɡeɪdʒmənt/ | n | sự đính hôn | an agreement to marry someone | They announced their engagement at dinner. |
| 2 | **engaged** | /ɪnˈɡeɪdʒd/ | adj | đã đính hôn | having agreed to marry someone | She is engaged to a doctor. |
| 3 | **fiancé** | /fiˈɒnseɪ/ | n | hôn phu | a man you are engaged to | Her fiancé works abroad. |
| 4 | **fiancée** | /fiˈɒnseɪ/ | n | hôn thê | a woman you are engaged to | He introduced his fiancée to us. |

| 8 | **bridegroom** | /ˈbraɪdɡruːm/ | n | chú rể | a man on his wedding day | The bridegroom looked nervous. |

| 11 | **wedding dress** | /ˈwedɪŋ dres/ | n | váy cưới | the dress a bride wears | She chose a simple wedding dress. |
| 12 | **honeymoon** | /ˈhʌnimuːn/ | n | tuần trăng mật | a holiday taken after a wedding | They went to Greece for their honeymoon. |

| 15 | **altar** | /ˈɔːltə(r)/ | n | bàn thờ | the table in a church used in a wedding | They stood at the altar together. |
| 16 | **bouquet** | /buˈkeɪ/ | n | bó hoa | a bunch of flowers, especially for a bride | She caught the bride's bouquet. |
| 17 | **toast** | /təʊst/ | n/v | lời chúc rượu; nâng ly | a speech wishing someone well | The father of the bride gave a toast. |
| 18 | **newlywed** | /ˈnjuːliwɛd/ | n | người mới cưới | a person who has recently married | The newlyweds moved into a small flat. |
| 19 | **marriage** | /ˈmærɪdʒ/ | n | hôn nhân | the state of being married | They have a happy marriage. |
| 20 | **marry** | /ˈmæri/ | v | kết hôn | to become someone's husband or wife | They decided to marry next year. |

| 16 | **loyalty** | /ˈlɔɪəlti/ | n | sự chung thủy | the quality of being faithful | Loyalty matters more than money to her. |
| 17 | **bond** | /bɒnd/ | n | sự gắn kết | a strong connection between people | There is a strong bond between the sisters. |
| 18 | **tolerate** | /ˈtɒləreɪt/ | v | chịu đựng, bao dung | to accept something you dislike | A good partner tolerates small differences. |
| 19 | **patience** | /ˈpeɪʃns/ | n | sự kiên nhẫn | the ability to wait calmly | Marriage requires patience. |
| 20 | **devotion** | /dɪˈvəʊʃn/ | n | sự tận tâm | deep love and loyalty | His devotion to his family is clear. |

| 13 | **unconditional** | /ˌʌnkənˈdɪʃənl/ | adj | vô điều kiện | given without conditions | A parent's love is often unconditional. |
| 14 | **fondness** | /ˈfɒndnəs/ | n | sự yêu mến | a gentle liking for someone | He has a fondness for old films. |
| 15 | **admire** | /ədˈmaɪə(r)/ | v | ngưỡng mộ | to respect and like someone | I admire her honesty. |
| 16 | **chemistry** | /ˈkemɪstri/ | n | sự hòa hợp | a strong feeling between two people | There was instant chemistry between them. |
| 17 | **compatible** | /kəmˈpætəbl/ | adj | hợp nhau | able to have a good relationship together | They are very compatible. |
| 18 | **tender** | /ˈtendə(r)/ | adj | dịu dàng | gentle and loving | He gave her a tender look. |
| 19 | **hug** | /hʌɡ/ | n/v | cái ôm; ôm | to hold someone closely | She gave her mother a long hug. |
| 20 | **kiss** | /kɪs/ | n/v | nụ hôn; hôn | to touch someone with your lips | He kissed her on the cheek. |

## Thành phố & nông thôn

`thanh-pho-nong-thon` · Từ vựng về đô thị, làng quê, tiện ích, so sánh hai nơi và quá trình đô thị hóa.

### Bài 1. Thành phố & tiện ích

_20 từ/cụm từ về khu vực đô thị, chính quyền thành phố, dân cư và tiện ích công cộng._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **urban** | /ˈɜːbən/ | adj | thuộc đô thị | relating to a town or city | Urban life can be exciting but noisy. |
| 2 | **downtown** | /ˌdaʊnˈtaʊn/ | n/adv | trung tâm thành phố | the main business area of a city | We spent the afternoon downtown. |
| 3 | **city centre** | /ˌsɪti ˈsentə(r)/ | n | trung tâm thành phố | the central part of a city | The museum is in the city centre. |
| 4 | **district** | /ˈdɪstrɪkt/ | n | quận, khu vực | an area of a town or city | They live in a quiet residential district. |
| 5 | **borough** | /ˈbʌrə/ | n | khu hành chính | a town or part of a city with its own council | The borough has its own library service. |
| 6 | **council** | /ˈkaʊnsl/ | n | hội đồng thành phố | the local government of a town | The council is repairing the roads. |
| 7 | **mayor** | /meə(r)/ | n | thị trưởng | the elected head of a city | The mayor gave a speech at the opening. |
| 8 | **resident** | /ˈrezɪdənt/ | n | cư dân | a person who lives in a place | Local residents complained about the noise. |
| 9 | **citizen** | /ˈsɪtɪzn/ | n | công dân | a person who legally belongs to a country or city | Every citizen has the right to vote. |
| 10 | **population** | /ˌpɒpjuˈleɪʃn/ | n | dân số | the number of people living in a place | The population has grown quickly. |
| 11 | **crowded** | /ˈkraʊdɪd/ | adj | đông đúc | full of people | The market is crowded on Saturdays. |
| 12 | **lively** | /ˈlaɪvli/ | adj | sôi động | full of energy and activity | The old quarter is lively at night. |
| 13 | **cosmopolitan** | /ˌkɒzməˈpɒlɪtən/ | adj | đa văn hóa | full of people from many countries | London is a cosmopolitan city. |
| 14 | **infrastructure** | /ˈɪnfrəstrʌktʃə(r)/ | n | cơ sở hạ tầng | the basic roads, services and systems of a place | The government is investing in infrastructure. |
| 15 | **facility** | /fəˈsɪləti/ | n | tiện ích | a place or service provided for a purpose | The sports facility is open to everyone. |
| 16 | **amenity** | /əˈmiːnəti/ | n | tiện nghi | a useful feature of a place | The area has good local amenities. |
| 17 | **cycle lane** | /ˈsaɪkl leɪn/ | n | làn xe đạp | a part of the road for bicycles | The new cycle lane makes commuting safer. |
| 18 | **plaza** | /ˈplɑːzə/ | n | quảng trường | a public square in a town | People gather in the plaza at weekends. |
| 19 | **fountain** | /ˈfaʊntən/ | n | đài phun nước | a structure that sends water into the air | We met by the fountain in the square. |
| 20 | **skyline** | /ˈskaɪlaɪn/ | n | đường chân trời | the outline of buildings against the sky | The skyline is full of tall towers. |

### Bài 2. Nông thôn & thiên nhiên

_20 từ/cụm từ về làng quê, đồng ruộng, sông hồ và công việc nông nghiệp._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **rural** | /ˈrʊərəl/ | adj | thuộc nông thôn | relating to the countryside | Rural areas often have fewer buses. |
| 2 | **countryside** | /ˈkʌntrisaɪd/ | n | vùng nông thôn | land outside towns and cities | We drove through beautiful countryside. |
| 3 | **village** | /ˈvɪlɪdʒ/ | n | ngôi làng | a small community in the countryside | She grew up in a fishing village. |

| 8 | **valley** | /ˈvæli/ | n | thung lũng | low land between hills | The river runs through the valley. |
| 9 | **hill** | /hɪl/ | n | đồi | a raised area of land, smaller than a mountain | Their house is on a small hill. |
| 10 | **stream** | /striːm/ | n | con suối | a small narrow river | A stream flows behind the cottage. |
| 11 | **pond** | /pɒnd/ | n | ao | a small area of still water | There are ducks on the pond. |
| 12 | **lake** | /leɪk/ | n | hồ | a large area of water surrounded by land | We swam in the lake every morning. |
| 13 | **forest** | /ˈfɒrɪst/ | n | rừng | a large area covered with trees | The forest is home to many animals. |
| 14 | **wood** | /wʊd/ | n | khu rừng nhỏ | a small forest | They walked through the wood. |
| 15 | **hedge** | /hedʒ/ | n | hàng rào cây | a line of bushes forming a barrier | He trimmed the hedge along the garden. |
| 16 | **fence** | /fens/ | n | hàng rào | a barrier made of wood or wire | The fence keeps the sheep inside. |
| 17 | **barn** | /bɑːn/ | n | kho thóc, chuồng trại | a large farm building for animals or crops | The hay is stored in the barn. |
| 18 | **farmhouse** | /ˈfɑːmhaʊs/ | n | nhà nông trại | the main house on a farm | They turned the old farmhouse into a hotel. |
| 19 | **tractor** | /ˈtræktə(r)/ | n | máy kéo | a powerful vehicle used on farms | The farmer drove the tractor across the field. |
| 20 | **windmill** | /ˈwɪndmɪl/ | n | cối xay gió | a building with sails turned by the wind | The old windmill is now a museum. |

### Bài 3. So sánh thành phố & nông thôn

_20 từ/cụm từ về nhịp sống, chi phí, cơ hội việc làm và ưu nhược điểm của hai nơi._

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 3 | **isolated** | /ˈaɪsəleɪtɪd/ | adj | biệt lập | separate and far from others | The farm is isolated in winter. |

| 8 | **close to nature** | /ˌkləʊs tə ˈneɪtʃə(r)/ | phrase | gần gũi thiên nhiên | living near natural surroundings | They moved to the countryside to be close to nature. |
| 9 | **opportunity** | /ˌɒpəˈtjuːnəti/ | n | cơ hội | a chance to do something | Cities offer more job opportunities. |
| 10 | **nightlife** | /ˈnaɪtlaɪf/ | n | cuộc sống về đêm | entertainment available at night | The city is famous for its nightlife. |
| 11 | **neighbourly** | /ˈneɪbəli/ | adj | thân thiện với hàng xóm | friendly towards neighbours | People here are very neighbourly. |
| 12 | **safe** | /seɪf/ | adj | an toàn | not likely to be harmed | The village feels safe at night. |
| 13 | **crime** | /kraɪm/ | n | tội phạm | illegal activity | Crime is lower in rural areas. |
| 14 | **crime rate** | /ˈkraɪm reɪt/ | n | tỷ lệ tội phạm | the number of crimes in an area | The crime rate has fallen this year. |
| 15 | **disadvantage** | /ˌdɪsədˈvɑːntɪdʒ/ | n | bất lợi | a condition that makes something harder | The main disadvantage is the long commute. |
| 16 | **advantage** | /ədˈvɑːntɪdʒ/ | n | lợi thế | something that helps you | One advantage of city life is public transport. |
| 17 | **migrate** | /maɪˈɡreɪt/ | v | di cư | to move from one place to another | Many families migrate to the city for work. |
| 18 | **migration** | /maɪˈɡreɪʃn/ | n | sự di cư | the movement of people to a new place | Migration from villages is increasing. |
| 19 | **settle** | /ˈsetl/ | v | định cư | to start living permanently in a place | They settled in a small town by the sea. |
| 20 | **pace of life** | /ˌpeɪs əv ˈlaɪf/ | n | nhịp sống | how fast daily life feels | The pace of life is slower in the countryside. |

### Bài 4. Đô thị hóa & quy hoạch

_20 từ/cụm từ về phát triển đô thị, quy hoạch, xây dựng và các vấn đề nhà ở._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 8 | **construction** | /kənˈstrʌkʃn/ | n | việc xây dựng | the work of building something | The construction of the bridge took three years. |
| 9 | **demolish** | /dɪˈmɒlɪʃ/ | v | phá dỡ | to destroy a building completely | They demolished the old cinema. |
| 10 | **renovate** | /ˈrenəveɪt/ | v | cải tạo | to repair and modernise a building | We renovated the kitchen last year. |
| 11 | **regenerate** | /rɪˈdʒenəreɪt/ | v | tái tạo | to improve an area that was in bad condition | The council wants to regenerate the old port. |
| 12 | **overcrowded** | /ˌəʊvəˈkraʊdɪd/ | adj | quá đông đúc | containing too many people | The buses are overcrowded at rush hour. |
| 13 | **shortage** | /ˈʃɔːtɪdʒ/ | n | sự thiếu hụt | a situation where there is not enough of something | There is a shortage of affordable homes. |
| 14 | **affordable** | /əˈfɔːdəbl/ | adj | giá phải chăng | cheap enough for ordinary people | The city needs more affordable housing. |
| 15 | **housing** | /ˈhaʊzɪŋ/ | n | nhà ở | buildings for people to live in | Housing prices have risen sharply. |
| 16 | **slum** | /slʌm/ | n | khu ổ chuột | a poor and overcrowded area | The charity works in the city's slums. |
| 17 | **relocate** | /ˌriːləʊˈkeɪt/ | v | di dời | to move to a different place | The families were relocated to new flats. |
| 18 | **green space** | /ˌɡriːn ˈspeɪs/ | n | không gian xanh | public land with grass and trees | Green space is important for city residents. |
| 19 | **pedestrianise** | /pəˈdestriənaɪz/ | v | dành cho người đi bộ | to make a street for pedestrians only | They pedestrianised the main shopping street. |
| 20 | **outskirts** | /ˈaʊtskɜːts/ | n | vùng ngoại vi | the outer parts of a town | They live on the outskirts of the city. |

## Động vật & thiên nhiên

`dong-vat-thien-nhien` · Từ vựng về động vật hoang dã, tập tính, thực vật, hệ sinh thái và công tác bảo tồn.

### Bài 1. Động vật hoang dã

_20 từ/cụm từ về các nhóm động vật và những loài phổ biến trên cạn và dưới biển._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **wildlife** | /ˈwaɪldlaɪf/ | n | động vật hoang dã | animals and plants living in nature | The island is famous for its wildlife. |
| 2 | **mammal** | /ˈmæml/ | n | động vật có vú | a warm-blooded animal that feeds its young milk | Dolphins are mammals. |
| 3 | **reptile** | /ˈreptaɪl/ | n | bò sát | a cold-blooded animal such as a snake | Snakes and lizards are reptiles. |
| 4 | **insect** | /ˈɪnsekt/ | n | côn trùng | a small animal with six legs | Bees are useful insects. |
| 5 | **amphibian** | /æmˈfɪbiən/ | n | động vật lưỡng cư | an animal that lives on land and in water | Frogs are amphibians. |
| 6 | **predator** | /ˈpredətə(r)/ | n | động vật ăn thịt | an animal that hunts others | The lion is a powerful predator. |

| 20 | **shark** | /ʃɑːk/ | n | cá mập | a large sea fish with sharp teeth | There have been shark sightings here. |

| 11 | **habitat** | /ˈhæbɪtæt/ | n | môi trường sống | the natural home of an animal or plant | The wetland is an important habitat. |

| 20 | **swamp** | /swɒmp/ | n | đầm lầy | an area of soft wet land | The swamp is full of insects. |

### Bài 4. Bảo tồn & quan sát

_20 từ/cụm từ về khu bảo tồn, quan sát động vật, nạn săn trộm và việc bảo vệ loài._

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 9 | **wilderness** | /ˈwɪldənəs/ | n | vùng hoang dã | a wild area with no people | They explored the wilderness for days. |
| 10 | **extinct** | /ɪkˈstɪŋkt/ | adj | tuyệt chủng | no longer existing | The dodo has been extinct for centuries. |
| 11 | **extinction** | /ɪkˈstɪŋkʃn/ | n | sự tuyệt chủng | the disappearance of a species | Hunting drove the species to extinction. |
| 12 | **protect** | /prəˈtekt/ | v | bảo vệ | to keep something safe | We must protect endangered animals. |
| 13 | **protection** | /prəˈtekʃn/ | n | sự bảo vệ | the act of keeping something safe | The law offers protection to rare species. |
| 14 | **poach** | /pəʊtʃ/ | v | săn trộm | to hunt animals illegally | Elephants are poached for their tusks. |

| 18 | **volunteer** | /ˌvɒlənˈtɪə(r)/ | n/v | tình nguyện viên; tình nguyện | a person who works without pay | She works as a volunteer at the sanctuary. |
| 19 | **donation** | /dəʊˈneɪʃn/ | n | sự quyên góp | money or goods given to help | The charity relies on public donations. |

## Giáo dục & tương lai

`giao-duc-tuong-lai` · Từ vựng về học tập suốt đời, kỹ năng học, hướng nghiệp và xu hướng giáo dục mới.

### Bài 1. Học tập suốt đời

_20 từ/cụm từ về kiến thức, kỹ năng, động lực học và các hình thức học ngoài trường._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **lifelong learning** | /ˌlaɪflɒŋ ˈlɜːnɪŋ/ | n | học tập suốt đời | learning that continues throughout life | Lifelong learning helps people adapt to change. |
| 2 | **skill** | /skɪl/ | n | kỹ năng | the ability to do something well | Communication is an important skill. |
| 3 | **knowledge** | /ˈnɒlɪdʒ/ | n | kiến thức | information and understanding about a subject | She has a lot of knowledge about history. |
| 4 | **ability** | /əˈbɪləti/ | n | khả năng | the power or skill to do something | He has the ability to learn quickly. |
| 5 | **talent** | /ˈtælənt/ | n | tài năng | a natural ability | She has a talent for drawing. |
| 6 | **potential** | /pəˈtenʃl/ | n | tiềm năng | the ability to develop in the future | The course helps students reach their potential. |
| 7 | **curiosity** | /ˌkjʊəriˈɒsəti/ | n | sự tò mò | a strong wish to learn about things | Curiosity is the start of learning. |
| 8 | **motivation** | /ˌməʊtɪˈveɪʃn/ | n | động lực | the reason or energy to do something | Praise can increase motivation. |
| 9 | **motivated** | /ˈməʊtɪveɪtɪd/ | adj | có động lực | wanting to do something | The students are highly motivated. |
| 10 | **target** | /ˈtɑːɡɪt/ | n | mục tiêu | a result you aim to achieve | My target is to read ten books this year. |
| 11 | **self-study** | /ˌself ˈstʌdi/ | n | tự học | learning without a teacher | Self-study requires discipline. |
| 12 | **online course** | /ˌɒnlaɪn ˈkɔːs/ | n | khóa học trực tuyến | a course taken over the internet | I finished an online course in web design. |
| 13 | **workshop** | /ˈwɜːkʃɒp/ | n | hội thảo thực hành | a practical class on a subject | She attended a writing workshop. |
| 14 | **tutorial** | /tjuːˈtɔːriəl/ | n | buổi hướng dẫn | a small teaching session | The tutorial covers grammar and writing. |
| 15 | **distance learning** | /ˈdɪstəns lɜːnɪŋ/ | n | học từ xa | studying without attending a school | Distance learning is popular with working adults. |
| 16 | **tuition** | /tjuˈɪʃn/ | n | học phí; sự giảng dạy | money paid for teaching | Tuition fees have increased this year. |
| 17 | **scholarship** | /ˈskɒləʃɪp/ | n | học bổng | money given to a student to study | She won a scholarship to a top university. |
| 18 | **grant** | /ɡrɑːnt/ | n | khoản trợ cấp | money given for a particular purpose | He received a research grant. |
| 19 | **enrolment** | /ɪnˈrəʊlmənt/ | n | sự ghi danh | the act of joining a course | Enrolment closes at the end of August. |
| 20 | **enrol** | /ɪnˈrəʊl/ | v | ghi danh | to join a course officially | She enrolled in a part-time degree. |

### Bài 2. Kỹ năng học tập

_20 từ/cụm từ về tập trung, quản lý thời gian, ghi nhớ và các phương pháp học hiệu quả._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **concentrate** | /ˈkɒnsntreɪt/ | v | tập trung | to give all your attention to something | I can't concentrate with the TV on. |
| 2 | **focus** | /ˈfəʊkəs/ | v/n | tập trung; sự tập trung | to direct your attention to something | Focus on one task at a time. |
| 3 | **organise** | /ˈɔːɡənaɪz/ | v | sắp xếp | to plan and arrange things | She organised her notes by topic. |
| 4 | **prioritise** | /praɪˈɒrətaɪz/ | v | ưu tiên | to decide what is most important | You need to prioritise your work. |
| 5 | **plan** | /plæn/ | v/n | lên kế hoạch; kế hoạch | to decide how to do something | We planned our revision schedule. |
| 6 | **manage time** | /ˌmænɪdʒ ˈtaɪm/ | phrase | quản lý thời gian | to use your time efficiently | Learning to manage time is essential at university. |
| 7 | **objective** | /əbˈdʒektɪv/ | n | mục tiêu | something you plan to achieve | The objective of the lesson is clear. |
| 8 | **strategy** | /ˈstrætədʒi/ | n | chiến lược | a plan for achieving something | She has a good strategy for exams. |
| 9 | **method** | /ˈmeθəd/ | n | phương pháp | a way of doing something | This method works well for vocabulary. |
| 10 | **technique** | /tekˈniːk/ | n | kỹ thuật | a particular way of doing something | Try a different study technique. |
| 11 | **practise** | /ˈpræktɪs/ | v | luyện tập | to do something regularly to improve | Practise speaking English every day. |
| 12 | **repetition** | /ˌrepəˈtɪʃn/ | n | sự lặp lại | doing the same thing many times | Repetition helps with memorisation. |
| 13 | **recall** | /rɪˈkɔːl/ | v | nhớ lại | to remember something | I couldn't recall the answer. |
| 14 | **comprehension** | /ˌkɒmprɪˈhenʃn/ | n | sự hiểu | the ability to understand something | The test checks reading comprehension. |
| 15 | **critical thinking** | /ˌkrɪtɪkl ˈθɪŋkɪŋ/ | n | tư duy phản biện | the ability to think carefully and question ideas | University encourages critical thinking. |
| 16 | **problem-solving** | /ˈprɒbləm sɒlvɪŋ/ | n | giải quyết vấn đề | the process of finding solutions | The course focuses on problem-solving. |
| 17 | **teamwork** | /ˈtiːmwɜːk/ | n | làm việc nhóm | working well with other people | Teamwork is important in most jobs. |
| 18 | **creativity** | /ˌkriːeɪˈtɪvəti/ | n | sự sáng tạo | the ability to produce new ideas | The project rewards creativity. |
| 19 | **confidence** | /ˈkɒnfɪdəns/ | n | sự tự tin | belief in your own ability | Speaking in class builds confidence. |
| 20 | **improvement** | /ɪmˈpruːvmənt/ | n | sự cải thiện | the process of getting better | There has been a clear improvement in her writing. |

### Bài 4. Tương lai giáo dục

_20 từ/cụm từ về công nghệ trong lớp học, đánh giá và xu hướng học tập mới._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 6 | **personalised** | /ˈpɜːsənaɪzd/ | adj | cá nhân hóa | designed for one person's needs | The app offers personalised exercises. |
| 7 | **interactive** | /ˌɪntərˈæktɪv/ | adj | tương tác | involving communication with the user | The museum has interactive displays. |
| 8 | **digital literacy** | /ˌdɪdʒɪtl ˈlɪtərəsi/ | n | kỹ năng số | the ability to use digital technology | Digital literacy is now a basic skill. |
| 9 | **robotics** | /rəʊˈbɒtɪks/ | n | ngành robot | the science of designing robots | The university offers a course in robotics. |
| 10 | **syllabus** | /ˈsɪləbəs/ | n | chương trình môn học | a list of topics in a course | The syllabus is available online. |
| 11 | **assessment** | /əˈsesmənt/ | n | sự đánh giá | the process of testing ability | Assessment includes essays and presentations. |
| 12 | **examination** | /ɪɡˌzæmɪˈneɪʃn/ | n | kỳ thi | a formal test of knowledge | The examination lasts three hours. |
| 13 | **credential** | /krəˈdenʃl/ | n | chứng chỉ, bằng cấp | a qualification that proves ability | Employers check candidates' credentials. |
| 14 | **academic** | /ˌækəˈdemɪk/ | adj | học thuật | relating to study and education | She has a strong academic background. |
| 15 | **vocational** | /vəʊˈkeɪʃənl/ | adj | hướng nghiệp | teaching skills for a particular job | Vocational training is practical and focused. |
| 16 | **practical** | /ˈpræktɪkl/ | adj | thực tiễn | useful in real situations | The course is more practical than theoretical. |
| 17 | **theoretical** | /ˌθɪəˈretɪkl/ | adj | lý thuyết | based on ideas rather than practice | The first term is mainly theoretical. |
| 18 | **critical** | /ˈkrɪtɪkl/ | adj | phê phán | carefully judging information | Critical reading is an important skill. |
| 19 | **independent** | /ˌɪndɪˈpendənt/ | adj | độc lập | able to work or learn alone | Older students are expected to be independent. |
| 20 | **lifelong** | /ˈlaɪflɒŋ/ | adj | suốt đời | continuing through your whole life | She has a lifelong interest in science. |

## Sức khỏe tinh thần & lối sống

`suc-khoe-tinh-than` · Từ vựng về cảm xúc, căng thẳng, thói quen tốt và cách chăm sóc sức khỏe tinh thần.

### Bài 1. Tâm trạng & sức khỏe tinh thần

_20 từ/cụm từ về cảm xúc, tâm lý, lo âu và các khái niệm sức khỏe tinh thần._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **mental** | /ˈmentl/ | adj | thuộc tinh thần | relating to the mind | Mental health is as important as physical health. |
| 2 | **psychological** | /ˌsaɪkəˈlɒdʒɪkl/ | adj | thuộc tâm lý | relating to the mind and feelings | The injury caused psychological stress. |
| 3 | **emotional** | /ɪˈməʊʃənl/ | adj | thuộc cảm xúc | relating to feelings | She needed emotional support. |
| 4 | **mood** | /muːd/ | n | tâm trạng | the way you feel at a particular time | Exercise improves my mood. |
| 5 | **mindset** | /ˈmaɪndset/ | n | tư duy | a way of thinking about things | A positive mindset makes problems easier. |
| 6 | **attitude** | /ˈætɪtjuːd/ | n | thái độ | the way you think and behave | Try to keep a healthy attitude to failure. |
| 7 | **resilience** | /rɪˈzɪliəns/ | n | khả năng phục hồi | the ability to recover from difficulties | Children need resilience to cope with change. |
| 8 | **burnout** | /ˈbɜːnaʊt/ | n | sự kiệt sức | extreme tiredness caused by too much work | She took a break to avoid burnout. |
| 9 | **anxiety** | /æŋˈzaɪəti/ | n | sự lo âu | a feeling of worry and fear | He suffers from anxiety before exams. |
| 10 | **depression** | /dɪˈpreʃn/ | n | trầm cảm | a serious feeling of sadness and hopelessness | Depression is a treatable condition. |
| 11 | **therapy** | /ˈθerəpi/ | n | liệu pháp | treatment for a mental or physical problem | She has been in therapy for a year. |
| 12 | **therapist** | /ˈθerəpɪst/ | n | nhà trị liệu | a person trained to treat mental health | The therapist listened without judging. |
| 13 | **counselling** | /ˈkaʊnsəlɪŋ/ | n | tư vấn tâm lý | professional advice about personal problems | The school offers free counselling. |
| 14 | **counsellor** | /ˈkaʊnsələ(r)/ | n | cố vấn tâm lý | a person who gives advice and support | Our counsellor helped us talk about stress. |
| 15 | **self-care** | /ˌself ˈkeə(r)/ | n | chăm sóc bản thân | looking after your own health and happiness | Self-care includes rest, food and exercise. |
| 16 | **mindfulness** | /ˈmaɪndflnəs/ | n | chánh niệm | the practice of paying attention to the present | Mindfulness can reduce anxiety. |
| 17 | **meditation** | /ˌmedɪˈteɪʃn/ | n | thiền | the practice of calming the mind | Ten minutes of meditation helps me focus. |
| 18 | **meditate** | /ˈmedɪteɪt/ | v | thiền định | to calm your mind through meditation | She meditates before work. |
| 19 | **breathing** | /ˈbriːðɪŋ/ | n | hơi thở | the act of taking air into the lungs | Deep breathing helps you relax. |
| 20 | **balance** | /ˈbæləns/ | n/v | sự cân bằng; cân bằng | a healthy mixture of different things | Try to balance work and rest. |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **bedtime** | /ˈbedtaɪm/ | n | giờ đi ngủ | the time when you go to bed | Try to keep a regular bedtime. |
| 2 | **nap** | /næp/ | n | giấc ngủ trưa | a short sleep during the day | I took a twenty-minute nap. |

| 5 | **recreation** | /ˌrekriˈeɪʃn/ | n | sự giải trí | activities done for enjoyment | The park is used for recreation. |

| 7 | **digital detox** | /ˌdɪdʒɪtl ˈdiːtɒks/ | n | cai nghiện thiết bị số | a period without phones or computers | She did a digital detox for a week. |

| 10 | **willpower** | /ˈwɪlpaʊə(r)/ | n | ý chí | the ability to control your actions | It takes willpower to stop scrolling. |
| 11 | **self-discipline** | /ˌself ˈdɪsəplɪn/ | n | tự kỷ luật | the ability to make yourself do things | Self-discipline helps you keep good habits. |
| 12 | **reward** | /rɪˈwɔːd/ | n/v | phần thưởng; thưởng | something given for good behaviour | Give yourself a small reward after studying. |
| 13 | **intention** | /ɪnˈtenʃn/ | n | ý định | something you plan to do | My intention is to exercise three times a week. |
| 14 | **priority** | /praɪˈɒrəti/ | n | sự ưu tiên | something that is more important | Make sleep a priority. |
| 15 | **screen-free** | /ˈskriːn friː/ | adj | không màn hình | without using phones or screens | We have a screen-free evening once a week. |
| 16 | **outdoors** | /ˌaʊtˈdɔːz/ | adv | ở ngoài trời | not inside a building | The children play outdoors after school. |
| 17 | **nature** | /ˈneɪtʃə(r)/ | n | thiên nhiên | the natural world | Walking in nature reduces stress. |
| 18 | **sunlight** | /ˈsʌnlaɪt/ | n | ánh sáng mặt trời | light from the sun | Sunlight helps your body make vitamin D. |
| 19 | **water intake** | /ˈwɔːtə ɪnteɪk/ | n | lượng nước nạp vào | the amount of water you drink | Increase your water intake in hot weather. |
| 20 | **balanced diet** | /ˌbælənst ˈdaɪət/ | n | chế độ ăn cân bằng | food that gives all the nutrients you need | A balanced diet supports good mental health. |

### Bài 3. Căng thẳng & quản lý

_20 từ/cụm từ về đối phó với áp lực, các dấu hiệu căng thẳng và cách tìm hỗ trợ._

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 2 | **cope with** | /ˈkəʊp wɪð/ | phrase | đối phó với | to manage a difficult situation | How do you cope with a heavy workload? |
| 3 | **manage** | /ˈmænɪdʒ/ | v | xoay xở, quản lý | to succeed in dealing with something | She manages her time well. |

| 10 | **migraine** | /ˈmaɪɡreɪn/ | n | chứng đau nửa đầu | a severe headache | Bright light makes her migraine worse. |
| 11 | **appetite** | /ˈæpɪtaɪt/ | n | sự thèm ăn | the desire to eat | Stress affected his appetite. |
| 12 | **comfort** | /ˈkʌmfət/ | n/v | sự an ủi; an ủi | a feeling of being less worried or sad | She found comfort in music. |
| 13 | **comfort zone** | /ˈkʌmfət zəʊn/ | n | vùng an toàn | a situation where you feel safe | Learning a language means leaving your comfort zone. |

| 16 | **confide** | /kənˈfaɪd/ | v | tâm sự | to tell someone a secret or problem | She confided in her sister. |
| 17 | **journal** | /ˈdʒɜːnl/ | n | nhật ký | a notebook for daily thoughts | He writes in a journal every night. |
| 18 | **journaling** | /ˈdʒɜːnəlɪŋ/ | n | việc viết nhật ký | the habit of writing a journal | Journaling helps her process emotions. |
| 19 | **gratitude** | /ˈɡrætɪtjuːd/ | n | lòng biết ơn | the feeling of being thankful | She practises gratitude every morning. |
| 20 | **positive** | /ˈpɒzətɪv/ | adj | tích cực | hopeful and constructive | Try to keep a positive attitude. |

### Bài 4. Chăm sóc & hỗ trợ

_20 từ/cụm từ về chẩn đoán, điều trị, phục hồi và sự đồng cảm với người gặp khó khăn._

| 12 | **addicted** | /əˈdɪktɪd/ | adj | nghiện | unable to stop using something | He became addicted to his phone. |
| 13 | **addiction** | /əˈdɪkʃn/ | n | sự nghiện | the inability to stop doing something harmful | Gambling addiction is a serious problem. |
| 14 | **rehab** | /ˈriːhæb/ | n | phục hồi chức năng | treatment to help someone recover | She spent a month in rehab. |
