## Giao tiếp trong công việc

`giao-tiep-cong-viec` · Từ vựng về trao đổi công việc, họp, thuyết trình, đàm phán và phản hồi hiệu quả.

### Bài 1. Giao tiếp nơi làm việc

_20 từ/cụm từ về email, thông báo, cách xưng hô và quy tắc giao tiếp công sở._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **communication** | /kəˌmjuːnɪˈkeɪʃn/ | n | sự giao tiếp | the exchange of information | Good communication prevents mistakes. |
| 2 | **memo** | /ˈmeməʊ/ | n | bản ghi nhớ | a short official note | The manager sent a memo about the new policy. |
| 3 | **brief** | /briːf/ | n/v | bản tóm tắt; giao việc | a short summary or set of instructions | The client gave us a brief for the project. |
| 4 | **briefing** | /ˈbriːfɪŋ/ | n | buổi giao ban | a meeting to give information | There is a briefing at nine o'clock. |
| 5 | **announcement** | /əˈnaʊnsmənt/ | n | thông báo | a public statement | The director made an announcement about the merger. |
| 6 | **circulate** | /ˈsɜːkjəleɪt/ | v | gửi vòng, phổ biến | to send information to a group | Please circulate the report to the team. |
| 7 | **cc** | /ˌsiː ˈsiː/ | v | gửi bản sao | to send a copy of an email to someone | Cc me on the message to finance. |
| 8 | **subject line** | /ˈsʌbdʒɪkt laɪn/ | n | dòng tiêu đề | the title of an email | Write a clear subject line. |
| 9 | **signature** | /ˈsɪɡnətʃə(r)/ | n | chữ ký | your name written at the end of a message | Add your job title to your email signature. |
| 10 | **informal** | /ɪnˈfɔːml/ | adj | thân mật | relaxed and not official | The team uses informal language in chat. |
| 11 | **polite** | /pəˈlaɪt/ | adj | lịch sự | showing good manners | Even a short email should be polite. |
| 12 | **concise** | /kənˈsaɪs/ | adj | ngắn gọn | short and clear | Keep your message concise. |
| 13 | **clarify** | /ˈklærəfaɪ/ | v | làm rõ | to make something easier to understand | Could you clarify the last point? |
| 14 | **confirm** | /kənˈfɜːm/ | v | xác nhận | to say that something is definitely true | Please confirm your attendance. |
| 15 | **respond** | /rɪˈspɒnd/ | v | phản hồi | to reply | She responded within an hour. |
| 16 | **query** | /ˈkwɪəri/ | n/v | câu hỏi; hỏi | a question about something | Send any queries to the HR team. |
| 17 | **input** | /ˈɪnpʊt/ | n | ý kiến đóng góp | advice or ideas you give | We welcome your input on the plan. |
| 18 | **chain of command** | /ˌtʃeɪn əv kəˈmɑːnd/ | n | hệ thống cấp bậc | the official line of authority | Report the problem through the chain of command. |
| 19 | **subordinate** | /səˈbɔːdɪnət/ | n | cấp dưới | a person with a lower position | She manages six subordinates. |
| 20 | **hierarchy** | /ˈhaɪərɑːki/ | n | hệ thống thứ bậc | a system where people have different ranks | The company has a flat hierarchy. |

### Bài 2. Họp & thuyết trình

_20 từ/cụm từ về chuẩn bị họp, thuyết trình, biên bản và đưa ra quyết định tập thể._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **slide** | /slaɪd/ | n | trang trình chiếu | one page of a presentation | The first slide shows the sales figures. |
| 2 | **projector** | /prəˈdʒektə(r)/ | n | máy chiếu | a machine that shows images on a screen | Could you turn on the projector? |
| 3 | **handout** | /ˈhændaʊt/ | n | tài liệu phát tay | a printed sheet given to people | The speaker prepared a handout for everyone. |
| 4 | **chairperson** | /ˈtʃeəpɜːsn/ | n | người chủ trì | the person who leads a meeting | The chairperson kept the discussion on track. |
| 5 | **minutes** | /ˈmɪnɪts/ | n | biên bản cuộc họp | the written record of a meeting | She emailed the minutes the next day. |
| 6 | **action point** | /ˈækʃn pɔɪnt/ | n | đầu việc cần làm | a task agreed in a meeting | We listed five action points. |
| 7 | **vote** | /vəʊt/ | n/v | sự bỏ phiếu; bỏ phiếu | a formal choice made by a group | The team voted on the new design. |
| 8 | **consensus** | /kənˈsensəs/ | n | sự đồng thuận | general agreement among a group | They reached a consensus after a long debate. |
| 9 | **brainstorm** | /ˈbreɪnstɔːm/ | v/n | động não | to suggest many ideas quickly | Let's brainstorm some solutions. |
| 10 | **suggest** | /səˈdʒest/ | v | đề xuất | to offer an idea | I suggest we start with the budget. |
| 11 | **suggestion** | /səˈdʒestʃən/ | n | lời đề xuất | an idea or plan offered | Thank you for your suggestion. |
| 12 | **object** | /əbˈdʒekt/ | v | phản đối | to say you disagree | Several members objected to the plan. |
| 13 | **interrupt** | /ˌɪntəˈrʌpt/ | v | ngắt lời | to stop someone while they are speaking | Please don't interrupt the speaker. |
| 14 | **digress** | /daɪˈɡres/ | v | lạc đề | to move away from the main subject | Let's not digress from the agenda. |
| 15 | **summarise** | /ˈsʌməraɪz/ | v | tóm tắt | to give the main points briefly | She summarised the discussion at the end. |
| 16 | **conclusion** | /kənˈkluːʒn/ | n | kết luận | the final decision or judgement | The meeting ended without a clear conclusion. |
| 17 | **elaborate** | /ɪˈlæbəreɪt/ | v | giải thích chi tiết | to add more detail | Could you elaborate on that point? |
| 18 | **persuade** | /pəˈsweɪd/ | v | thuyết phục | to make someone agree | He persuaded the team to try a new approach. |
| 19 | **convince** | /kənˈvɪns/ | v | thuyết phục | to make someone believe something | Her argument convinced everyone. |
| 20 | **unanimous** | /juˈnænɪməs/ | adj | nhất trí | agreed by everyone | The board made a unanimous decision. |

### Bài 3. Đàm phán & giải quyết vấn đề

_20 từ/cụm từ về thỏa thuận, nhượng bộ, xung đột và cách tìm giải pháp chung._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **negotiation** | /nɪˌɡəʊʃiˈeɪʃn/ | n | sự đàm phán | discussion to reach an agreement | The negotiation lasted all afternoon. |
| 2 | **deal** | /diːl/ | n | thỏa thuận | an agreement, especially in business | They finally closed the deal. |
| 3 | **terms** | /tɜːmz/ | n | điều khoản | the conditions of an agreement | Read the terms before you sign. |
| 4 | **agreement** | /əˈɡriːmənt/ | n | thỏa thuận | an arrangement accepted by both sides | The two companies reached an agreement. |
| 5 | **clause** | /klɔːz/ | n | điều khoản hợp đồng | one part of a legal document | The contract has a confidentiality clause. |
| 6 | **offer** | /ˈɒfə(r)/ | n/v | đề nghị | something you say you will give | They made us a generous offer. |
| 7 | **counter-offer** | /ˈkaʊntər ɒfə(r)/ | n | đề nghị đối lại | a reply to an offer with different terms | She responded with a counter-offer. |
| 8 | **concession** | /kənˈseʃn/ | n | sự nhượng bộ | something you agree to give up | Both sides made concessions. |
| 9 | **dispute** | /dɪˈspjuːt/ | n/v | tranh chấp | a serious disagreement | The dispute was settled out of court. |
| 10 | **conflict** | /ˈkɒnflɪkt/ | n | xung đột | a serious disagreement | There is conflict between the two departments. |
| 11 | **resolve** | /rɪˈzɒlv/ | v | giải quyết | to solve a problem | We resolved the issue quickly. |
| 12 | **resolution** | /ˌrezəˈluːʃn/ | n | sự giải quyết | the act of solving a problem | They reached a peaceful resolution. |
| 13 | **mediator** | /ˈmiːdieɪtə(r)/ | n | người hòa giải | a person who helps others agree | An independent mediator was brought in. |
| 14 | **mediate** | /ˈmiːdieɪt/ | v | hòa giải | to help two sides reach agreement | She mediated between the manager and the staff. |
| 15 | **escalate** | /ˈeskəleɪt/ | v | leo thang | to become more serious | The complaint escalated to senior management. |
| 16 | **solution** | /səˈluːʃn/ | n | giải pháp | a way to solve a problem | We need a practical solution. |
| 17 | **issue** | /ˈɪʃuː/ | n | vấn đề | a subject or problem being discussed | The main issue is the delivery date. |
| 18 | **urgent** | /ˈɜːdʒənt/ | adj | khẩn cấp | needing immediate attention | This is an urgent matter. |
| 19 | **alternative** | /ɔːlˈtɜːnətɪv/ | n/adj | lựa chọn thay thế | another possibility | We need an alternative supplier. |
| 20 | **outcome** | /ˈaʊtkʌm/ | n | kết quả | the final result | The outcome of the talks was positive. |

### Bài 4. Phản hồi & đánh giá

_20 từ/cụm từ về khen ngợi, góp ý, đánh giá hiệu suất và ghi nhận thành tích._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **evaluation** | /ɪˌvæljuˈeɪʃn/ | n | sự đánh giá | a careful judgement of something | The project is due for evaluation. |
| 2 | **evaluate** | /ɪˈvæljueɪt/ | v | đánh giá | to judge the value or quality | We evaluate staff performance twice a year. |
| 3 | **constructive** | /kənˈstrʌktɪv/ | adj | mang tính xây dựng | helpful and intended to improve | Thank you for the constructive comments. |
| 4 | **criticism** | /ˈkrɪtɪsɪzəm/ | n | sự phê bình | negative comments about something | She accepted the criticism calmly. |
| 5 | **praise** | /preɪz/ | n/v | lời khen; khen | words expressing approval | The manager praised her hard work. |
| 6 | **recognition** | /ˌrekəɡˈnɪʃn/ | n | sự ghi nhận | public appreciation of achievement | He received recognition for his contribution. |
| 7 | **achievement** | /əˈtʃiːvmənt/ | n | thành tích | something done successfully | Winning the contract was a major achievement. |
| 8 | **weakness** | /ˈwiːknəs/ | n | điểm yếu | a fault or lack of strength | Lack of planning is his main weakness. |
| 9 | **milestone** | /ˈmaɪlstəʊn/ | n | cột mốc | an important stage in a process | The launch was an important milestone. |
| 10 | **productivity** | /ˌprɒdʌkˈtɪvəti/ | n | năng suất | the rate at which work is done | Flexible hours increased productivity. |
| 11 | **efficient** | /ɪˈfɪʃnt/ | adj | hiệu quả | working well without waste | The new system is more efficient. |
| 12 | **effective** | /ɪˈfektɪv/ | adj | hiệu lực, hiệu quả | producing the intended result | The training was very effective. |
| 13 | **incentive** | /ɪnˈsentɪv/ | n | ưu đãi, động lực | something that encourages you to work harder | Bonuses are an incentive to perform well. |
| 14 | **pay rise** | /ˈpeɪ raɪz/ | n | sự tăng lương | an increase in salary | She asked for a pay rise. |
| 15 | **probation** | /prəˈbeɪʃn/ | n | thời gian thử việc | a trial period in a new job | New staff are on probation for three months. |
| 16 | **mentoring** | /ˈmentɔːrɪŋ/ | n | việc cố vấn | advice and support from an experienced person | The company runs a mentoring programme. |
| 17 | **coaching** | /ˈkəʊtʃɪŋ/ | n | huấn luyện | training to improve a specific skill | He received coaching before the presentation. |
| 18 | **capability** | /ˌkeɪpəˈbɪləti/ | n | năng lực | the ability to do something | The team has the capability to deliver. |
| 19 | **competence** | /ˈkɒmpɪtəns/ | n | năng lực chuyên môn | the ability to do a job well | We assess technical competence in the interview. |
| 20 | **accountability** | /əˌkaʊntəˈbɪləti/ | n | trách nhiệm giải trình | being responsible for your actions | Clear roles improve accountability. |

## Sự kiện & lễ hội

`su-kien-le-hoi` · Từ vựng về lễ hội, phong tục truyền thống, cách tổ chức và tham dự sự kiện, cùng các buổi lễ kỷ niệm và trao giải.

### Bài 1. Lễ hội & ngày lễ

_20 từ/cụm từ về lễ hội, phong tục, nghi lễ và các hoạt động truyền thống trong năm._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **fair** | /feə(r)/ | n | hội chợ | a public event with stalls and entertainment | The village fair is held every August. |
| 2 | **festivity** | /feˈstɪvəti/ | n | lễ hội, không khí lễ hội | the happy feeling of a celebration | Colourful lights added to the festivity. |
| 3 | **carnival** | /ˈkɑːnɪvl/ | n | hội hóa trang, lễ hội đường phố | a public festival with music, costumes and dancing | The carnival fills the streets with dancers. |
| 4 | **parade** | /pəˈreɪd/ | n | cuộc diễu hành | a public procession to celebrate something | We watched the parade from the pavement. |
| 5 | **fireworks** | /ˈfaɪəwɜːks/ | n | pháo hoa | explosive objects that make coloured lights in the sky | The fireworks lit up the whole harbour. |
| 6 | **costume** | /ˈkɒstjuːm/ | n | trang phục (hóa trang, biểu diễn) | clothes worn for a festival or performance | She made her own costume for the parade. |
| 7 | **folklore** | /ˈfəʊklɔː(r)/ | n | văn hóa dân gian | the traditional stories and beliefs of a community | The book collects folklore from the region. |
| 8 | **superstition** | /ˌsuːpəˈstɪʃn/ | n | mê tín | a belief that is not based on reason | It is a superstition that black cats bring bad luck. |
| 9 | **ritual** | /ˈrɪtʃuəl/ | n | nghi lễ | a set of actions performed in a fixed way | The tea ceremony is an important ritual. |
| 10 | **rite** | /raɪt/ | n | nghi thức | a formal ceremony | The rite is performed at the start of spring. |
| 11 | **holiday** | /ˈhɒlədeɪ/ | n | ngày lễ, kỳ nghỉ | a day or period when people do not work | The school is closed for the holiday. |
| 12 | **public holiday** | /ˌpʌblɪk ˈhɒlədeɪ/ | n | ngày nghỉ lễ | a day when most businesses close | The first of May is a public holiday. |
| 13 | **eve** | /iːv/ | n | đêm trước ngày lễ | the day or evening before an important day | We always have a big dinner on New Year's Eve. |
| 14 | **decorate** | /ˈdekəreɪt/ | v | trang trí | to make something look more attractive | They decorate the square with lights and flowers. |
| 15 | **lantern** | /ˈlæntən/ | n | đèn lồng | a light inside a frame, often carried at festivals | Children carried paper lanterns through the streets. |
| 16 | **procession** | /prəˈseʃn/ | n | đoàn rước, đám rước | a line of people moving as part of a ceremony | The procession moved slowly towards the temple. |
| 17 | **legend** | /ˈledʒənd/ | n | truyền thuyết | an old story about famous people or events | The legend explains how the lake was formed. |
| 18 | **commemorate** | /kəˈmeməreɪt/ | v | tưởng niệm, kỷ niệm | to remember and honour an important event or person | The monument commemorates the end of the war. |
| 19 | **festive** | /ˈfestɪv/ | adj | mang không khí lễ hội | typical of a celebration | The streets had a festive atmosphere. |
| 20 | **annual** | /ˈænjuəl/ | adj | hằng năm | happening once every year | The annual festival takes place in June. |

### Bài 2. Tổ chức sự kiện

_20 từ/cụm từ về địa điểm, khách mời, ngân sách, hậu cần và các bước chuẩn bị cho một sự kiện._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **occasion** | /əˈkeɪʒn/ | n | dịp, sự kiện | a particular time when something happens | The wedding was a happy occasion. |
| 2 | **auditorium** | /ˌɔːdɪˈtɔːriəm/ | n | khán phòng | the part of a building where the audience sits | The auditorium seats five hundred people. |
| 3 | **organiser** | /ˈɔːɡənaɪzə(r)/ | n | người tổ chức | a person who plans and arranges an event | The organiser sent us the final schedule. |
| 4 | **name badge** | /ˈneɪm bædʒ/ | n | thẻ tên | a small card showing a person's name | Please collect your name badge at the desk. |
| 5 | **guest list** | /ˈɡest lɪst/ | n | danh sách khách mời | a list of people invited to an event | Please check your name on the guest list. |
| 6 | **seating** | /ˈsiːtɪŋ/ | n | chỗ ngồi | the arrangement of seats for guests | The seating is arranged in rows. |
| 7 | **catering** | /ˈkeɪtərɪŋ/ | n | dịch vụ ăn uống | the business of providing food for events | The catering includes a vegetarian option. |
| 8 | **refreshment** | /rɪˈfreʃmənt/ | n | đồ ăn uống nhẹ | light food and drinks | Refreshments will be served after the talk. |
| 9 | **decoration** | /ˌdekəˈreɪʃn/ | n | đồ trang trí | something used to make a place look attractive | We hung decorations from the ceiling. |
| 10 | **programme** | /ˈprəʊɡræm/ | n | chương trình | a plan of activities for an event | The programme lists every speaker and time. |
| 11 | **expenses** | /ɪkˈspensɪz/ | n | chi phí | the money needed for an activity | We must keep the event expenses low. |
| 12 | **sponsor** | /ˈspɒnsə(r)/ | n/v | nhà tài trợ; tài trợ | a person or company that pays for an event | A local bank agreed to sponsor the race. |
| 13 | **time slot** | /ˈtaɪm slɒt/ | n | khung giờ | a period booked for a particular activity | Choose a time slot for your visit. |
| 14 | **set aside** | /ˌset əˈsaɪd/ | phr v | dành riêng | to keep something for a particular purpose | Set aside a small amount for emergencies. |
| 15 | **equipment** | /ɪˈkwɪpmənt/ | n | thiết bị | the tools and machines needed for an activity | All the sound equipment arrived on time. |
| 16 | **set up** | /ˌset ˈʌp/ | phr v | chuẩn bị, lắp đặt | to prepare equipment or a place | The team set up the stage in two hours. |
| 17 | **permit** | /ˈpɜːmɪt/ | n | giấy phép | an official document allowing something | You need a permit to close the street. |
| 18 | **safety** | /ˈseɪfti/ | n | an toàn | the state of being protected from danger | Safety checks were carried out before the event. |
| 19 | **sound system** | /ˈsaʊnd sɪstəm/ | n | hệ thống âm thanh | equipment for playing music and speech | The sound system was too loud at the back. |
| 20 | **run-through** | /ˈrʌn θruː/ | n | buổi chạy thử | a practice session before a performance | The final run-through went smoothly. |

### Bài 3. Tham dự sự kiện

_20 từ/cụm từ về khách mời, người chủ trì, xếp hàng, biểu diễn và cảm xúc khi tham dự._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **visitor** | /ˈvɪzɪtə(r)/ | n | khách tham quan | a person who visits a place or event | Visitors must sign in at reception. |
| 2 | **presenter** | /prɪˈzentə(r)/ | n | người dẫn chương trình | a person who introduces a show or event | The presenter kept the audience entertained. |
| 3 | **admission** | /ədˈmɪʃn/ | n | sự cho vào, phí vào cửa | the right or price to enter a place | Admission to the museum is free on Sundays. |
| 4 | **entrance** | /ˈentrəns/ | n | lối vào | the door or gate where you enter | We waited by the main entrance. |
| 5 | **turnstile** | /ˈtɜːnstaɪl/ | n | cổng xoay | a gate that turns as one person passes through | Fans entered the stadium through the turnstiles. |
| 6 | **crowd** | /kraʊd/ | n | đám đông | a large group of people in one place | A huge crowd gathered in the square. |
| 7 | **onlooker** | /ˈɒnlʊkə(r)/ | n | người xem, người đứng nhìn | a person who watches something happening | Onlookers gathered to watch the parade. |
| 8 | **meet-up** | /ˈmiːt ʌp/ | n | buổi gặp mặt | an informal meeting arranged in advance | We organised a meet-up for former classmates. |
| 9 | **matinee** | /ˈmætɪneɪ/ | n | suất diễn ban ngày | an afternoon performance | The Saturday matinee is popular with families. |
| 10 | **encore** | /ˈɒŋkɔː(r)/ | n | màn diễn thêm | an extra performance requested by the audience | The singer returned for an encore. |
| 11 | **interval** | /ˈɪntəvl/ | n | giờ nghỉ giữa buổi | a short break during a performance | We bought drinks during the interval. |
| 12 | **backstage** | /ˌbækˈsteɪdʒ/ | adv/n | sau sân khấu | the area behind the stage | The actors waited backstage before the show. |
| 13 | **autograph** | /ˈɔːtəɡrɑːf/ | n | chữ ký lưu niệm | a famous person's signature | She asked the author for an autograph. |
| 14 | **memento** | /məˈmentəʊ/ | n | vật kỷ niệm | a small object kept to remember an event | He kept the ticket as a memento of the concert. |
| 15 | **booth** | /buːð/ | n | quầy, gian hàng | a small temporary shop at an event | Local farmers sold cheese at a booth. |
| 16 | **showcase** | /ˈʃəʊkeɪs/ | n/v | buổi trưng bày; giới thiệu | an event that displays something to the public | The gallery held a showcase of young artists. |
| 17 | **display** | /dɪˈspleɪ/ | n/v | màn trưng bày; trưng bày | an arrangement of things for people to see | There was a display of traditional costumes. |
| 18 | **march** | /mɑːtʃ/ | n/v | cuộc tuần hành; diễu hành | an organized walk by a group of people | The march ended in the main square. |
| 19 | **applause** | /əˈplɔːz/ | n | tràng vỗ tay | the sound of many people clapping | The speech ended with loud applause. |
| 20 | **cheer** | /tʃɪə(r)/ | v/n | reo hò, cổ vũ | to shout loudly to show support | The fans cheered when the team appeared. |

### Bài 4. Lễ kỷ niệm & trao giải

_20 từ/cụm từ về lễ kỷ niệm, họp mặt, giải thưởng, đề cử và vinh danh thành tích._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **commemoration** | /kəˌmeməˈreɪʃn/ | n | sự tưởng niệm | an event that honours an important person or event | A commemoration was held at the war memorial. |
| 2 | **remembrance** | /rɪˈmembrəns/ | n | sự tưởng nhớ | the act of remembering and honouring someone | The service was a day of remembrance. |
| 3 | **jubilee** | /ˈdʒuːbɪliː/ | n | lễ kỷ niệm lớn | a special anniversary celebration | The village celebrated its golden jubilee. |
| 4 | **centenary** | /senˈtiːnəri/ | n | lễ kỷ niệm trăm năm | a 100th anniversary | The club marked its centenary with a dinner. |
| 5 | **homecoming** | /ˈhəʊmkʌmɪŋ/ | n | buổi trở về thăm | an event when former members return to a place | The school holds a homecoming every five years. |
| 6 | **banquet** | /ˈbæŋkwɪt/ | n | bữa tiệc long trọng | a formal meal for many people | The award ceremony ended with a banquet. |
| 7 | **speech** | /spiːtʃ/ | n | bài phát biểu | a formal talk given to an audience | The director gave a short speech. |
| 8 | **tribute** | /ˈtrɪbjuːt/ | n | lời tri ân | something said or done to show respect | The concert was a tribute to the composer. |
| 9 | **memorial** | /məˈmɔːriəl/ | n | đài tưởng niệm | a structure built to remember someone | They laid flowers at the memorial. |
| 10 | **award** | /əˈwɔːd/ | n/v | giải thưởng; trao giải | a prize given for achievement | She won an award for her research. |
| 11 | **prize** | /praɪz/ | n | giải thưởng | something given to the winner | The first prize was a trip to Paris. |
| 12 | **accolade** | /ˈækəleɪd/ | n | vinh dự, giải thưởng | praise or an award for achievement | The film received accolades from critics. |
| 13 | **plaque** | /plɑːk/ | n | tấm biển vinh danh | a flat piece of metal or stone with a name on it | A plaque honours the founder of the school. |
| 14 | **nominee** | /ˌnɒmɪˈniː/ | n | người được đề cử | a person officially suggested for an award | The nominees waited for the result. |
| 15 | **nomination** | /ˌnɒmɪˈneɪʃn/ | n | sự đề cử | the act of officially suggesting someone | She received a nomination for best actress. |
| 16 | **champion** | /ˈtʃæmpiən/ | n | nhà vô địch | the person who wins a competition | The champion gave a short thank-you speech. |
| 17 | **jury** | /ˈdʒʊəri/ | n | ban giám khảo | a group who decide the result of a competition | The jury praised all five finalists. |
| 18 | **honour** | /ˈɒnə(r)/ | n/v | vinh dự; tôn vinh | a sign of respect or recognition | It was an honour to receive the prize. |
| 19 | **recipient** | /rɪˈsɪpiənt/ | n | người nhận | a person who receives something | The recipient thanked the whole team. |
| 20 | **congratulation** | /kənˌɡrætʃuˈleɪʃn/ | n | lời chúc mừng | words said to praise someone's success | She sent a message of congratulation. |

## Môi trường & phát triển bền vững

`moi-truong-ben-vung` · Từ vựng về ô nhiễm, biến đổi khí hậu, năng lượng tái tạo, tiêu dùng bền vững và các chính sách bảo vệ môi trường.

### Bài 1. Ô nhiễm & rác thải

_20 từ/cụm từ về các loại ô nhiễm, rác thải, hóa chất độc hại và cách xử lý chất thải._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **contamination** | /kənˌtæmɪˈneɪʃn/ | n | sự nhiễm bẩn | the state of being polluted | The city has high levels of air contamination. |
| 2 | **poison** | /ˈpɔɪzn/ | v/n | đầu độc; chất độc | to make water or land harmful | The factory poisoned the river for years. |
| 3 | **debris** | /ˈdebriː/ | n | mảnh vụn, rác thải | pieces of waste left after damage | Debris from the storm covered the beach. |
| 4 | **fly-tipping** | /ˈflaɪ tɪpɪŋ/ | n | việc đổ rác trái phép | illegally leaving waste in a public place | Fly-tipping costs the council thousands of pounds. |
| 5 | **landfill** | /ˈlændfɪl/ | n | bãi chôn lấp | a place where waste is buried underground | Most of that plastic goes to landfill. |
| 6 | **discharge** | /ˈdɪstʃɑːdʒ/ | n/v | sự xả thải; xả thải | the release of gas or liquid into the environment | The new law limits carbon discharge. |
| 7 | **toxic** | /ˈtɒksɪk/ | adj | độc hại | containing poison | The site is full of toxic chemicals. |
| 8 | **chemical** | /ˈkemɪkl/ | n/adj | hóa chất; hóa học | a substance used in chemistry or industry | Farmers use fewer chemicals now. |
| 9 | **sewage** | /ˈsuːɪdʒ/ | n | nước thải | waste water and human waste carried away by pipes | Untreated sewage flowed into the sea. |
| 10 | **plastic** | /ˈplæstɪk/ | n/adj | nhựa | a light artificial material | Plastic takes hundreds of years to break down. |
| 11 | **disposable** | /dɪˈspəʊzəbl/ | adj | dùng một lần | made to be thrown away after use | Many cafés have stopped using disposable cups. |
| 12 | **upcycle** | /ˌʌpˈsaɪkl/ | v | tái chế nâng cấp | to turn old items into something more valuable | She upcycles old furniture and sells it online. |
| 13 | **recycling** | /ˌriːˈsaɪklɪŋ/ | n | việc tái chế | the process of treating waste to use again | Recycling reduces the amount of rubbish. |
| 14 | **bin** | /bɪn/ | n | thùng rác | a container for rubbish | Put the bottles in the recycling bin. |
| 15 | **dump** | /dʌmp/ | n/v | bãi rác; đổ, vứt | a place for rubbish or to put waste somewhere | They dump old furniture by the road. |
| 16 | **contaminate** | /kənˈtæmɪneɪt/ | v | làm ô nhiễm | to make something dirty or unsafe | The chemicals contaminated the groundwater. |
| 17 | **smog** | /smɒɡ/ | n | sương khói | air pollution mixed with fog | Thick smog covered the city centre. |
| 18 | **methane** | /ˈmiːθeɪn/ | n | khí metan | a powerful greenhouse gas | Farm animals produce methane. |
| 19 | **carbon-neutral** | /ˌkɑːbən ˈnjuːtrəl/ | adj | trung hòa carbon | producing no net increase in carbon dioxide | The company aims to become carbon-neutral by 2030. |
| 20 | **exhaust** | /ɪɡˈzɔːst/ | n | khí xả | gas produced by an engine | Car exhaust is a major cause of smog. |

### Bài 2. Khí hậu & năng lượng

_20 từ/cụm từ về biến đổi khí hậu, thời tiết cực đoan, nhiên liệu hóa thạch và năng lượng tái tạo._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **atmosphere** | /ˈætməsfɪə(r)/ | n | khí quyển | the layer of air around the earth | Pollution is damaging the atmosphere. |
| 2 | **ozone layer** | /ˈəʊzəʊn leɪə(r)/ | n | tầng ô-dôn | a layer of gas that protects the earth from the sun | The ozone layer is slowly recovering. |
| 3 | **global warming** | /ˌɡləʊbl ˈwɔːmɪŋ/ | n | sự nóng lên toàn cầu | the rise in the earth's average temperature | Global warming is melting the ice caps. |
| 4 | **greenhouse effect** | /ˈɡriːnhaʊs ɪfekt/ | n | hiệu ứng nhà kính | the trapping of heat in the atmosphere | The greenhouse effect keeps the planet warm. |
| 5 | **renewable** | /rɪˈnjuːəbl/ | adj | tái tạo | able to be replaced naturally | Wind and solar are renewable sources. |
| 6 | **solar** | /ˈsəʊlə(r)/ | adj | thuộc năng lượng mặt trời | relating to the sun's energy | They installed solar panels on the roof. |
| 7 | **wind turbine** | /ˈwɪnd tɜːbaɪn/ | n | tua-bin gió | a machine that turns wind into electricity | The wind turbines stand along the coast. |
| 8 | **crude oil** | /ˌkruːd ˈɔɪl/ | n | dầu thô | oil in its natural state before processing | Crude oil is used to make petrol. |
| 9 | **coal** | /kəʊl/ | n | than đá | a black rock burned as fuel | The old power station burned coal. |
| 10 | **water shortage** | /ˈwɔːtə ʃɔːtɪdʒ/ | n | sự thiếu nước | a lack of enough water for people's needs | The region faces a serious water shortage. |
| 11 | **flash flood** | /ˈflæʃ flʌd/ | n | lũ quét | a sudden local flood | The storm caused flash floods in the valley. |
| 12 | **blaze** | /bleɪz/ | n | đám cháy lớn | a large fire that burns strongly | Firefighters fought the blaze for hours. |
| 13 | **glacier** | /ˈɡlæsiə(r)/ | n | sông băng | a large mass of ice that moves slowly | The glacier has shrunk dramatically. |
| 14 | **coastline** | /ˈkəʊstlaɪn/ | n | đường bờ biển | the edge of the land next to the sea | Erosion is changing the coastline. |
| 15 | **logging** | /ˈlɒɡɪŋ/ | n | việc khai thác gỗ | the cutting down of trees for wood | Logging has damaged the forest. |
| 16 | **carbon dioxide** | /ˌkɑːbən daɪˈɒksaɪd/ | n | khí carbon dioxide | the gas produced when carbon is burned | Trees absorb carbon dioxide from the air. |
| 17 | **low-carbon** | /ˌləʊ ˈkɑːbən/ | adj | ít carbon | producing only a small amount of carbon dioxide | We need low-carbon forms of transport. |
| 18 | **generate** | /ˈdʒenəreɪt/ | v | sản xuất (điện, năng lượng) | to produce electricity or energy | The dam generates electricity for the region. |
| 19 | **hydropower** | /ˈhaɪdrəʊpaʊə(r)/ | n | thủy điện | electricity produced from moving water | Hydropower supplies a third of the country's energy. |
| 20 | **biomass** | /ˈbaɪəʊmæs/ | n | sinh khối | plant or animal material used as fuel | Biomass is an alternative to coal. |

### Bài 3. Tiêu dùng & lối sống bền vững

_20 từ/cụm từ về tiêu dùng có trách nhiệm, sản phẩm thân thiện môi trường và thói quen sống xanh._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **consumption** | /kənˈsʌmpʃn/ | n | sự tiêu thụ | the use of goods, energy or food | Energy consumption falls in warmer months. |
| 2 | **shopper** | /ˈʃɒpə(r)/ | n | người mua sắm | a person who buys goods | Shoppers are choosing greener products. |
| 3 | **environmentally friendly** | /ɪnˌvaɪrənˈmentəli ˈfrendli/ | adj | thân thiện môi trường | not harmful to the environment | The hotel uses environmentally friendly cleaning products. |
| 4 | **organic** | /ɔːˈɡænɪk/ | adj | hữu cơ | grown without artificial chemicals | Organic fruit is often more expensive. |
| 5 | **local produce** | /ˌləʊkl ˈprɒdjuːs/ | n | nông sản địa phương | food grown near where it is sold | The market sells fresh local produce. |
| 6 | **pre-loved** | /ˌpriː ˈlʌvd/ | adj | đồ cũ (đã qua sử dụng) | previously owned by someone else | She bought a pre-loved bicycle. |
| 7 | **reuse** | /ˌriːˈjuːz/ | v | tái sử dụng | to use something again | Reuse your shopping bags. |
| 8 | **reduce** | /rɪˈdjuːs/ | v | giảm thiểu | to make something smaller in amount | We should reduce the amount of paper we use. |
| 9 | **recondition** | /ˌriːkənˈdɪʃn/ | v | tân trang, phục hồi | to repair something so it works like new | It is cheaper to recondition the old machine. |
| 10 | **package** | /ˈpækɪdʒ/ | v/n | đóng gói; gói hàng | to wrap goods for sale or transport | The soap is packaged in paper. |
| 11 | **packaging** | /ˈpækɪdʒɪŋ/ | n | bao bì | the material used to wrap goods | The company reduced its plastic packaging. |
| 12 | **fair trade** | /ˌfeə ˈtreɪd/ | n | thương mại công bằng | trade that gives producers a fair price | This coffee is certified fair trade. |
| 13 | **ethical** | /ˈeθɪkl/ | adj | có đạo đức, có trách nhiệm | morally right and fair | She only buys from ethical brands. |
| 14 | **conserve** | /kənˈsɜːv/ | v | tiết kiệm, bảo tồn | to protect something from being wasted or lost | Turn off the tap to conserve water. |
| 15 | **efficiency** | /ɪˈfɪʃnsi/ | n | hiệu quả | the quality of working well without waste | New appliances improve energy efficiency. |
| 16 | **minimal** | /ˈmɪnɪməl/ | adj | tối thiểu | very small in amount | The building has a minimal impact on the site. |
| 17 | **energy-saving** | /ˈenədʒi seɪvɪŋ/ | adj | tiết kiệm năng lượng | designed to use less electricity | They installed energy-saving lights. |
| 18 | **refill** | /ˌriːˈfɪl/ | v/n | đổ đầy lại; lần đổ đầy | to fill a container again | You can refill your water bottle here. |
| 19 | **compost** | /ˈkɒmpɒst/ | n/v | phân hữu cơ; ủ phân | decayed plant material used to improve soil | We compost vegetable scraps in the garden. |
| 20 | **durable** | /ˈdjʊərəbl/ | adj | bền | lasting a long time without breaking | Durable clothes are better for the planet. |

### Bài 4. Chính sách & hành động cộng đồng

_20 từ/cụm từ về luật môi trường, chiến dịch cộng đồng, tài trợ và biện pháp bảo vệ hệ sinh thái._

| # | Từ/cụm từ | IPA | Loại | Nghĩa (VI) | Nghĩa (EN) | Ví dụ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **mandate** | /ˈmændeɪt/ | n | chỉ thị, chính sách | an official instruction or policy | The council adopted a new recycling mandate. |
| 2 | **regulation** | /ˌreɡjuˈleɪʃn/ | n | quy định | an official rule | New regulations limit factory emissions. |
| 3 | **legislation** | /ˌledʒɪsˈleɪʃn/ | n | pháp luật | laws made by a government | The legislation bans single-use plastics. |
| 4 | **treaty** | /ˈtriːti/ | n | hiệp ước | a formal agreement between countries | The treaty sets targets for reducing emissions. |
| 5 | **summit** | /ˈsʌmɪt/ | n | hội nghị thượng đỉnh | a meeting of world leaders | Climate talks dominated the summit. |
| 6 | **activist** | /ˈæktɪvɪst/ | n | nhà hoạt động | a person who campaigns for change | Activists blocked the entrance to the site. |
| 7 | **campaign** | /kæmˈpeɪn/ | n/v | chiến dịch; vận động | a series of actions to achieve a goal | They launched a campaign against plastic bags. |
| 8 | **petition** | /pəˈtɪʃn/ | n | kiến nghị | a written request signed by many people | Ten thousand people signed the petition. |
| 9 | **protest** | /ˈprəʊtest/ | n/v | cuộc phản đối; phản đối | a public show of disagreement | There was a peaceful protest outside the factory. |
| 10 | **initiative** | /ɪˈnɪʃətɪv/ | n | sáng kiến | a new plan to solve a problem | The tree-planting initiative started last spring. |
| 11 | **scheme** | /skiːm/ | n | chương trình, kế hoạch | an official plan or arrangement | The council runs a bike-sharing scheme. |
| 12 | **subsidy** | /ˈsʌbsədi/ | n | trợ cấp | money paid by a government to support something | Subsidies make solar panels cheaper. |
| 13 | **funding** | /ˈfʌndɪŋ/ | n | nguồn tài trợ | money provided for a project | The project needs more public funding. |
| 14 | **charity** | /ˈtʃærəti/ | n | tổ chức từ thiện | an organization that helps people or nature | The charity plants trees in city parks. |
| 15 | **civic** | /ˈsɪvɪk/ | adj | thuộc cộng đồng, công dân | relating to a town and its people | Civic pride inspired the clean-up campaign. |
| 16 | **stewardship** | /ˈstjuːədʃɪp/ | n | sự gìn giữ, quản lý có trách nhiệm | the responsible care of something valuable | She works in wildlife stewardship. |
| 17 | **preserve** | /prɪˈzɜːv/ | v | gìn giữ, bảo vệ | to keep something in its original state | We must preserve these wetlands. |
| 18 | **restore** | /rɪˈstɔː(r)/ | v | phục hồi | to return something to a good condition | Volunteers restored the damaged riverbank. |
| 19 | **penalty** | /ˈpenəlti/ | n | hình phạt | a punishment for breaking a rule | Companies face heavy penalties for illegal dumping. |
| 20 | **enforce** | /ɪnˈfɔːs/ | v | thực thi | to make people obey a law or rule | It is difficult to enforce the ban. |

## Ghi chú kiểm tra

- 2000 từ, không trùng lặp trong nội bộ A2.
- A1 và A2 có thể dùng chung từ nền tảng; mỗi cấp vẫn được kiểm tra trùng riêng.
- Đủ 6 trường: từ, IPA, từ loại, nghĩa Việt, nghĩa Anh, ví dụ.
- Mỗi bài đúng 20 từ; mỗi chủ đề 4 bài.
